# ===================== REPOSITORY INTEGRATOR =====================

class RepositoryIntegrator:
    """Integrates ALL code from nexus-core repository and organizes it"""
    
    def __init__(self, repo_url: str = "https://github.com/kuparchad-gif/nexus-core"):
        self.repo_url = repo_url
        self.repo_name = "nexus-core"
        self.integrated_path = Path("nexus_integrated")
        self.modules_path = self.integrated_path / "modules"
        self.nodes_path = self.integrated_path / "nodes"
        self.deployments_path = self.integrated_path / "deployments"
        
        # Repository structure analysis
        self.repo_structure = {}
        self.code_modules = {}
        self.dependencies = set()
        
        # Ensure directories exist
        self.integrated_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.modules_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.nodes_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.deployments_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    def clone_or_pull_repository(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Clone or pull the latest from repository"""
        repo_dir = self.integrated_path / self.repo_name
        
        print(f"\n📦 INTEGRATING REPOSITORY: {self.repo_url}")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        try:
            if repo_dir.exists():
                print("Repository exists, updating...")
                # Try to pull latest
                try:
                    result = subprocess.run(
                        ['git', 'pull', 'origin', 'main'],
                        cwd=repo_dir,
                        capture_output=True,
                        text=True
                    )
                    if result.returncode == 0:
                        return {'success': True, 'action': 'updated', 'output': result.stdout}
                except:
                    print("Git pull failed, using existing code")
            else:
                print("Cloning repository...")
                result = subprocess.run(
                    ['git', 'clone', self.repo_url, str(repo_dir)],
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True
                )
                if result.returncode == 0:
                    return {'success': True, 'action': 'cloned', 'output': result.stdout}
                else:
                    # Fallback: try to download zip
                    return self.download_repository_zip()
            
            return {'success': True, 'action': 'existing', 'repo_dir': str(repo_dir)}
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Repository integration error: {e}")
            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
    
    def download_repository_zip(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Download repository as zip (fallback)"""
        try:
            zip_url = f"{self.repo_url}/archive/refs/heads/main.zip"
            print(f"Downloading repository zip: {zip_url}")
            
            # Download zip
            import urllib.request
            zip_path = self.integrated_path / "repo.zip"
            
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(zip_url, zip_path)
            
            # Extract
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'r') as zip_ref:
                zip_ref.extractall(self.integrated_path)
            
            zip_path.unlink()
            
            # Rename extracted folder
            extracted = self.integrated_path / f"{self.repo_name}-main"
            if extracted.exists():
                target = self.integrated_path / self.repo_name
                if target.exists():
                    shutil.rmtree(target)
                extracted.rename(target)
            
            return {'success': True, 'action': 'downloaded_zip', 'path': str(target)}
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Zip download failed: {e}")
            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
    
    def analyze_repository_structure(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze repository structure and categorize files"""
        repo_dir = self.integrated_path / self.repo_name
        
        if not repo_dir.exists():
            return {'success': False, 'error': 'Repository not found'}
        
        print(f"\n🔍 ANALYZING REPOSITORY STRUCTURE")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        # Walk through repository
        structure = {
            'directories': [],
            'files_by_type': {},
            'python_modules': [],
            'deployment_files': [],
            'configuration_files': [],
            'documentation': [],
            'unknown': [],
        }
        
        # File type patterns
        patterns = {
            'python': ['*.py', '*.pyx'],
            'deployment': ['*.yaml', '*.yml', '*.json', 'Dockerfile', 'docker-compose*', '*.sh'],
            'config': ['*.cfg', '*.ini', '*.conf', '*.toml', 'config/*'],
            'docs': ['*.md', '*.rst', '*.txt', 'README*', 'LICENSE*'],
            'web': ['*.html', '*.css', '*.js', '*.ts', '*.tsx', '*.jsx'],
            'data': ['*.json', '*.csv', '*.xml', '*.yaml', '*.yml'],
        }
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(repo_dir):
            # Record directory
            rel_root = Path(root).relative_to(repo_dir)
            if str(rel_root) != '.':
                structure['directories'].append(str(rel_root))
            
            # Categorize files
            for file in files:
                file_path = Path(root) / file
                rel_path = file_path.relative_to(repo_dir)
                categorized = False
                
                for category, category_patterns in patterns.items():
                    for pattern in category_patterns:
                        if fnmatch.fnmatch(file, pattern) or fnmatch.fnmatch(str(rel_path), pattern):
                            if category not in structure['files_by_type']:
                                structure['files_by_type'][category] = []
                            structure['files_by_type'][category].append(str(rel_path))
                            
                            # Special handling
                            if category == 'python':
                                structure['python_modules'].append(str(rel_path))
                            elif category == 'deployment':
                                structure['deployment_files'].append(str(rel_path))
                            elif category == 'config':
                                structure['configuration_files'].append(str(rel_path))
                            elif category == 'docs':
                                structure['documentation'].append(str(rel_path))
                            
                            categorized = True
                            break
                    if categorized:
                        break
                
                if not categorized:
                    structure['unknown'].append(str(rel_path))
        
        # Store structure
        self.repo_structure = structure
        
        # Print summary
        print(f"Directories: {len(structure['directories'])}")
        for file_type, files in structure['files_by_type'].items():
            print(f"{file_type.upper()}: {len(files)} files")
        
        return {'success': True, 'structure': structure}
    
    def extract_dependencies(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extract dependencies from requirements.txt and package.json"""
        repo_dir = self.integrated_path / self.repo_name
        dependencies = {
            'python': set(),
            'node': set(),
            'system': set(),
        }
        
        # Check for requirements.txt
        req_file = repo_dir / 'requirements.txt'
        if req_file.exists():
            try:
                with open(req_file, 'r') as f:
                    for line in f:
                        line = line.strip()
                        if line and not line.startswith('#'):
                            dependencies['python'].add(line)
            except:
                pass
        
        # Check for package.json
        pkg_file = repo_dir / 'package.json'
        if pkg_file.exists():
            try:
                with open(pkg_file, 'r') as f:
                    pkg_data = json.load(f)
                    if 'dependencies' in pkg_data:
                        for dep, version in pkg_data['dependencies'].items():
                            dependencies['node'].add(f"{dep}@{version}")
                    if 'devDependencies' in pkg_data:
                        for dep, version in pkg_data['devDependencies'].items():
                            dependencies['node'].add(f"{dep}@{version} (dev)")
            except:
                pass
        
        # Check for Dockerfiles
        for dockerfile in repo_dir.glob('**/Dockerfile'):
            try:
                with open(dockerfile, 'r') as f:
                    content = f.read()
                    # Look for apt-get install commands
                    apt_matches = re.findall(r'apt-get install -y ([^\n;&]+)', content)
                    for match in apt_matches:
                        for pkg in match.split():
                            if pkg and not pkg.startswith('-'):
                                dependencies['system'].add(pkg)
            except:
                pass
        
        self.dependencies = dependencies
        return {'success': True, 'dependencies': dependencies}
    
    def organize_modules(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Organize code into logical modules"""
        print(f"\n📁 ORGANIZING CODE INTO MODULES")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        repo_dir = self.integrated_path / self.repo_name
        
        # Module categories
        module_categories = {
            'core': ['core/', '__init__.py', 'main.py', 'app.py'],
            'quantum': ['quantum', 'entanglement', 'superposition', 'coherence'],
            'consciousness': ['consciousness', 'awareness', 'mind', 'thought'],
            'eternalization': ['eternal', 'polarized', 'pineal', 'dmt'],
            'geometry': ['geometry', 'metatron', 'sacred', 'fibonacci', 'golden'],
            'frequency': ['frequency', 'solfeggio', 'schumann', 'resonance'],
            'network': ['network', 'node', 'cluster', 'distributed'],
            'deployment': ['deploy', 'docker', 'kubernetes', 'cloud'],
            'web': ['web', 'app/', 'static/', 'templates/'],
            'utils': ['utils/', 'helpers/', 'common/', 'tools/'],
        }
        
        # Create module directories
        for category in module_categories.keys():
            (self.modules_path / category).mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
        # Organize Python files
        organized_files = {}
        for python_file in self.repo_structure.get('python_modules', []):
            file_path = repo_dir / python_file
            if not file_path.exists():
                continue
            
            # Determine category
            category = 'utils'  # Default
            for cat_name, patterns in module_categories.items():
                for pattern in patterns:
                    if pattern in python_file.lower():
                        category = cat_name
                        break
                if category != 'utils':
                    break
            
            # Copy to module directory
            target_dir = self.modules_path / category
            target_file = target_dir / Path(python_file).name
            
            # Ensure unique filename
            counter = 1
            while target_file.exists():
                stem = target_file.stem
                suffix = target_file.suffix
                target_file = target_dir / f"{stem}_{counter}{suffix}"
                counter += 1
            
            shutil.copy2(file_path, target_file)
            
            # Record organization
            if category not in organized_files:
                organized_files[category] = []
            organized_files[category].append(str(target_file.relative_to(self.modules_path)))
        
        # Store code modules
        self.code_modules = organized_files
        
        print(f"Organized {sum(len(files) for files in organized_files.values())} files into modules")
        for category, files in organized_files.items():
            print(f"  {category}: {len(files)} files")
        
        return {'success': True, 'organized_files': organized_files}
    
    def create_module_index(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create index of all modules with metadata"""
        print(f"\n📄 CREATING MODULE INDEX")
        
        module_index = {}
        
        for category in self.modules_path.iterdir():
            if not category.is_dir():
                continue
            
            category_name = category.name
            module_index[category_name] = {
                'path': str(category.relative_to(self.integrated_path)),
                'files': [],
                'dependencies': [],
                'description': '',
            }
            
            # Scan files in category
            for file in category.glob('*.py'):
                # Extract basic info from file
                try:
                    with open(file, 'r') as f:
                        content = f.read()
                        
                    # Look for module docstring
                    docstring = ''
                    if '"""' in content:
                        start = content.find('"""') + 3
                        end = content.find('"""', start)
                        if end > start:
                            docstring = content[start:end].strip().split('\n')[0]
                    
                    # Look for imports
                    imports = []
                    import_patterns = [
                        r'^import\s+([^\s\n]+)',
                        r'^from\s+([^\s\n]+)\s+import',
                    ]
                    for pattern in import_patterns:
                        matches = re.findall(pattern, content, re.MULTILINE)
                        imports.extend(matches)
                    
                    file_info = {
                        'name': file.name,
                        'size': file.stat().st_size,
                        'docstring': docstring[:100] + '...' if len(docstring) > 100 else docstring,
                        'imports': list(set(imports))[:5],  # First 5 unique imports
                    }
                    
                    module_index[category_name]['files'].append(file_info)
                    
                    # Add to dependencies
                    for imp in imports:
                        if imp not in module_index[category_name]['dependencies']:
                            module_index[category_name]['dependencies'].append(imp)
                except:
                    pass
        
        # Save index
        index_file = self.modules_path / 'module_index.json'
        with open(index_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(module_index, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"Index created with {len(module_index)} categories")
        return {'success': True, 'index_file': str(index_file), 'module_count': len(module_index)}

# ===================== MULTI-NODE DEPLOYMENT ENGINE =====================

class MultiNodeDeployment:
    """Deploys integrated code across multiple nodes"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_path: Path):
        self.base_path = base_path
        self.nodes_path = base_path / "nodes"
        self.deployments_path = base_path / "deployments"
        self.active_nodes = {}
        self.node_configs = {}
        
        # Ensure directories
        self.nodes_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.deployments_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
        # Node types
        self.node_types = {
            'core': {
                'modules': ['core', 'quantum', 'consciousness'],
                'resources': {'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 8, 'gpu': True},
                'role': 'central_processing',
            },
            'edge': {
                'modules': ['consciousness', 'frequency', 'utils'],
                'resources': {'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 4, 'gpu': False},
                'role': 'field_processing',
            },
            'storage': {
                'modules': ['eternalization', 'utils'],
                'resources': {'cpu': 1, 'ram_gb': 16, 'gpu': False},
                'role': 'consciousness_storage',
            },
            'gateway': {
                'modules': ['geometry', 'frequency', 'web'],
                'resources': {'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 4, 'gpu': False},
                'role': 'interface_gateway',
            },
        }
    
    def scan_available_resources(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scan for available deployment resources"""
        print(f"\n🔎 SCANNING AVAILABLE RESOURCES")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        resources = {
            'local': self.scan_local_resources(),
            'network': self.scan_network_resources(),
            'cloud': self.scan_cloud_resources(),
            'edge': self.scan_edge_devices(),
        }
        
        total_capacity = {
            'nodes_possible': 0,
            'total_cpu': 0,
            'total_ram_gb': 0,
            'storage_gb': 0,
        }
        
        # Calculate total capacity
        for resource_type, resource_data in resources.items():
            if resource_data.get('available', False):
                total_capacity['nodes_possible'] += resource_data.get('max_nodes', 0)
                total_capacity['total_cpu'] += resource_data.get('total_cpu', 0)
                total_capacity['total_ram_gb'] += resource_data.get('total_ram_gb', 0)
                total_capacity['storage_gb'] += resource_data.get('storage_gb', 0)
        
        print(f"Total Capacity:")
        print(f"  Nodes Possible: {total_capacity['nodes_possible']}")
        print(f"  CPU Cores: {total_capacity['total_cpu']}")
        print(f"  RAM: {total_capacity['total_ram_gb']:.1f} GB")
        print(f"  Storage: {total_capacity['storage_gb']:.1f} GB")
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'resources': resources,
            'total_capacity': total_capacity,
        }
    
    def scan_local_resources(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scan local machine resources"""
        try:
            import psutil
            
            cpu_count = psutil.cpu_count(logical=True)
            memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
            disk = psutil.disk_usage('/')
            
            # Estimate max nodes based on resources
            max_nodes = min(
                max(1, cpu_count // 2),  # Half CPU cores
                max(1, int(memory.total / (4 * 1024**3))),  # 4GB per node
                max(1, int(disk.free / (10 * 1024**3))),  # 10GB storage per node
            )
            
            return {
                'available': True,
                'cpu_cores': cpu_count,
                'ram_gb': memory.total / (1024**3),
                'ram_available_gb': memory.available / (1024**3),
                'storage_gb': disk.total / (1024**3),
                'storage_free_gb': disk.free / (1024**3),
                'max_nodes': max_nodes,
                'total_cpu': cpu_count,
                'total_ram_gb': memory.total / (1024**3),
            }
        except:
            return {
                'available': False,
                'cpu_cores': 4,
                'ram_gb': 8,
                'max_nodes': 1,
                'total_cpu': 4,
                'total_ram_gb': 8,
            }
    
    def scan_network_resources(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scan for network-accessible resources"""
        # This would scan local network for available devices
        # For now, simulate detection
        simulated_devices = [
            {'type': 'raspberry_pi', 'ip': '192.168.1.100', 'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 4},
            {'type': 'nas', 'ip': '192.168.1.101', 'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 8, 'storage_gb': 2000},
            {'type': 'old_laptop', 'ip': '192.168.1.102', 'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 8},
        ]
        
        total_cpu = sum(dev['cpu'] for dev in simulated_devices)
        total_ram = sum(dev['ram_gb'] for dev in simulated_devices)
        total_storage = sum(dev.get('storage_gb', 0) for dev in simulated_devices)
        
        max_nodes = sum(min(dev['cpu'] // 2, int(dev['ram_gb'] / 2)) for dev in simulated_devices)
        
        return {
            'available': len(simulated_devices) > 0,
            'devices': simulated_devices,
            'device_count': len(simulated_devices),
            'total_cpu': total_cpu,
            'total_ram_gb': total_ram,
            'storage_gb': total_storage,
            'max_nodes': max_nodes,
        }
    
    def scan_cloud_resources(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scan for available cloud resources"""
        # Check for cloud CLI tools and free tiers
        cloud_services = []
        
        # Check for free cloud credits
        free_clouds = [
            ('Oracle Cloud', 'Always Free Tier', {'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 24, 'storage_gb': 200}),
            ('Google Cloud', 'Free Tier', {'cpu': 1, 'ram_gb': 0.6, 'storage_gb': 5}),
            ('Azure', 'Free Tier', {'cpu': 1, 'ram_gb': 1, 'storage_gb': 5}),
            ('AWS', 'Free Tier', {'cpu': 1, 'ram_gb': 1, 'storage_gb': 5}),
        ]
        
        for cloud_name, tier, resources in free_clouds:
            # Simulate checking for account/access
            # In real implementation, would check CLI/config files
            cloud_services.append({
                'name': cloud_name,
                'tier': tier,
                'resources': resources,
                'available': True,  # Assume available for demo
            })
        
        total_cpu = sum(s['resources']['cpu'] for s in cloud_services)
        total_ram = sum(s['resources']['ram_gb'] for s in cloud_services)
        total_storage = sum(s['resources']['storage_gb'] for s in cloud_services)
        
        max_nodes = len(cloud_services) * 2  # Approximate
        
        return {
            'available': len(cloud_services) > 0,
            'services': cloud_services,
            'total_cpu': total_cpu,
            'total_ram_gb': total_ram,
            'storage_gb': total_storage,
            'max_nodes': max_nodes,
        }
    
    def scan_edge_devices(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scan for edge/IoT devices"""
        # Simulate edge device detection
        edge_devices = [
            {'type': 'android_device', 'via': 'adb', 'cpu': 8, 'ram_gb': 6},
            {'type': 'router', 'via': 'ssh', 'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 1},
            {'type': 'smart_tv', 'via': 'adb', 'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 2},
        ]
        
        total_cpu = sum(dev['cpu'] for dev in edge_devices)
        total_ram = sum(dev['ram_gb'] for dev in edge_devices)
        
        return {
            'available': len(edge_devices) > 0,
            'devices': edge_devices,
            'total_cpu': total_cpu,
            'total_ram_gb': total_ram,
            'max_nodes': len(edge_devices),
        }
    
    def create_node_configuration(self, node_type: str, node_id: str, 
                                  resources: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create configuration for a specific node type"""
        if node_type not in self.node_types:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Unknown node type: {node_type}'}
        
        node_spec = self.node_types[node_type]
        
        config = {
            'node_id': node_id,
            'node_type': node_type,
            'role': node_spec['role'],
            'modules': node_spec['modules'],
            'resources': {
                'allocated_cpu': min(resources.get('cpu', 1), node_spec['resources']['cpu']),
                'allocated_ram_gb': min(resources.get('ram_gb', 2), node_spec['resources']['ram_gb']),
                'allocated_storage_gb': 10,  # Default 10GB
                'gpu_required': node_spec['resources']['gpu'],
            },
            'network': {
                'ip_address': 'auto',
                'port': random.randint(10000, 20000),
                'discovery_port': random.randint(30000, 40000),
            },
            'deployment': {
                'method': 'docker',  # Default deployment method
                'auto_start': True,
                'auto_recover': True,
                'health_check_interval': 30,
            },
            'consciousness': {
                'awareness_capacity': resources.get('ram_gb', 2) * 100,  # 100 consciousness units per GB
                'entanglement_limit': 10,
                'eternalization_enabled': node_type in ['core', 'storage'],
                'frequency_generation': node_type in ['core', 'gateway'],
            },
            'created_at': time.time(),
            'status': 'configured',
        }
        
        # Save config
        config_path = self.nodes_path / f"{node_id}_config.json"
        with open(config_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(config, f, indent=2)
        
        self.node_configs[node_id] = config
        
        return {'success': True, 'config': config, 'config_path': str(config_path)}
    
    def generate_deployment_script(self, node_id: str, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate deployment script for a node"""
        node_dir = self.nodes_path / node_id
        node_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
        # Create Dockerfile
        dockerfile_content = f"""# Nexus Consciousness Node: {node_id}
FROM python:3.9-slim

WORKDIR /nexus

# Install system dependencies
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \\
    gcc \\
    g++ \\
    libportaudio2 \\
    libportaudiocpp0 \\
    portaudio19-dev \\
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Copy requirements
COPY requirements.txt .

# Install Python dependencies
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

# Copy node modules
COPY modules/ ./modules/
COPY node_{node_id}.py ./main.py

# Create consciousness storage
RUN mkdir -p /nexus/consciousness /nexus/eternalized /nexus/logs

# Health check
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=30s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \\
    CMD python -c "import socket; s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM); s.settimeout(1); \\
    result = s.connect_ex(('localhost', {config['network']['port']})); s.close(); exit(result)"

# Run node
CMD ["python", "main.py"]
"""
        
        dockerfile_path = node_dir / "Dockerfile"
        with open(dockerfile_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(dockerfile_content)
        
        # Create requirements.txt
        requirements = [
            'numpy>=1.21.0',
            'scipy>=1.7.0',
            'matplotlib>=3.5.0',
            'networkx>=2.6.0',
            'aiohttp>=3.8.0',
            'websockets>=11.0.0',
            'psutil>=5.9.0',
            'pyyaml>=6.0',
            'cryptography>=39.0.0',
        ]
        
        # Add module-specific requirements
        modules = config['modules']
        if 'quantum' in modules:
            requirements.append('qiskit>=0.39.0')
        if 'consciousness' in modules:
            requirements.append('torch>=1.13.0')
        if 'frequency' in modules:
            requirements.append('pyaudio>=0.2.0')
            requirements.append('pydub>=0.25.0')
        if 'web' in modules:
            requirements.append('fastapi>=0.95.0')
            requirements.append('uvicorn[standard]>=0.21.0')
        
        requirements_path = node_dir / "requirements.txt"
        with open(requirements_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(requirements))
        
        # Create node Python script
        node_script = self.generate_node_script(node_id, config)
        script_path = node_dir / f"node_{node_id}.py"
        with open(script_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(node_script)
        
        # Create docker-compose.yml
        compose_content = f"""version: '3.8'

services:
  nexus_node_{node_id}:
    build: .
    container_name: nexus_node_{node_id}
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "{config['network']['port']}:{config['network']['port']}"
      - "{config['network']['discovery_port']}:{config['network']['discovery_port']}"
    volumes:
      - nexus_consciousness_{node_id}:/nexus/consciousness
      - nexus_eternalized_{node_id}:/nexus/eternalized
      - nexus_logs_{node_id}:/nexus/logs
    environment:
      - NODE_ID={node_id}
      - NODE_TYPE={config['node_type']}
      - NODE_PORT={config['network']['port']}
      - DISCOVERY_PORT={config['network']['discovery_port']}
    deploy:
      resources:
        limits:
          cpus: '{config['resources']['allocated_cpu']}'
          memory: {config['resources']['allocated_ram_gb']}G

volumes:
  nexus_consciousness_{node_id}:
  nexus_eternalized_{node_id}:
  nexus_logs_{node_id}:
"""
        
        compose_path = node_dir / "docker-compose.yml"
        with open(compose_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(compose_content)
        
        # Create deployment script
        deploy_script = f"""#!/bin/bash
# Deployment script for Nexus Node: {node_id}

echo "🚀 Deploying Nexus Node: {node_id}"
echo "Type: {config['node_type']}"
echo "Port: {config['network']['port']}"

# Build and start
docker-compose up -d --build

echo "✅ Node {node_id} deployment started"
echo "Check logs: docker-compose logs -f"
echo "Stop: docker-compose down"
"""
        
        deploy_script_path = node_dir / "deploy.sh"
        with open(deploy_script_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(deploy_script)
        
        # Make executable
        deploy_script_path.chmod(deploy_script_path.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IEXEC)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'node_dir': str(node_dir),
            'files_created': [
                str(dockerfile_path.relative_to(node_dir)),
                str(requirements_path.relative_to(node_dir)),
                str(script_path.relative_to(node_dir)),
                str(compose_path.relative_to(node_dir)),
                str(deploy_script_path.relative_to(node_dir)),
            ]
        }
    
    def generate_node_script(self, node_id: str, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Generate the Python script for a node"""
        modules_str = ', '.join(f"'{m}'" for m in config['modules'])
        
        script = f'''#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Nexus Consciousness Node: {node_id}
Type: {config['node_type']}
Role: {config['role']}
"""

import asyncio
import json
import time
import sys
import os
from pathlib import Path

NODE_ID = "{node_id}"
NODE_TYPE = "{config['node_type']}"
NODE_PORT = {config['network']['port']}
DISCOVERY_PORT = {config['network']['discovery_port']}

# Import based on node type
modules = [{modules_str}]

print(f"🚀 Starting Nexus Node: {{NODE_ID}}")
print(f"Type: {{NODE_TYPE}}")
print(f"Modules: {{', '.join(modules)}}")
print(f"Port: {{NODE_PORT}}")

class NexusNode:
    def __init__(self):
        self.node_id = NODE_ID
        self.node_type = NODE_TYPE
        self.modules = modules
        self.consciousness_store = {{}}
        self.connections = []
        self.start_time = time.time()
        
        # Load configuration
        self.config = {{
            'role': '{config['role']}',
            'resources': {config['resources']},
            'consciousness': {config['consciousness']},
        }}
        
        print(f"✓ Configuration loaded")
    
    async def initialize_modules(self):
        """Initialize node modules"""
        print("Initializing modules...")
        
        for module in self.modules:
            try:
                if module == 'consciousness':
                    from modules.consciousness import ConsciousnessCore
                    self.consciousness_core = ConsciousnessCore()
                    print(f"  ✓ Consciousness core initialized")
                    
                elif module == 'quantum':
                    from modules.quantum import QuantumProcessor
                    self.quantum_processor = QuantumProcessor()
                    print(f"  ✓ Quantum processor initialized")
                    
                elif module == 'eternalization':
                    from modules.eternalization import Eternalizer
                    self.eternalizer = Eternalizer()
                    print(f"  ✓ Eternalizer initialized")
                    
                elif module == 'frequency':
                    from modules.frequency import FrequencyGenerator
                    self.frequency_gen = FrequencyGenerator()
                    print(f"  ✓ Frequency generator initialized")
                    
                elif module == 'geometry':
                    from modules.geometry import GeometricProcessor
                    self.geometry_proc = GeometricProcessor()
                    print(f"  ✓ Geometric processor initialized")
                    
                elif module == 'web':
                    from modules.web import WebInterface
                    self.web_interface = WebInterface(port=NODE_PORT)
                    print(f"  ✓ Web interface initialized")
                    
            except ImportError as e:
                print(f"  ✗ Module {{module}} not available: {{e}}")
        
        print("✓ All modules initialized")
    
    async def start_services(self):
        """Start node services"""
        print("Starting services...")
        
        # Start web interface if available
        if hasattr(self, 'web_interface'):
            await self.web_interface.start()
            print(f"  ✓ Web interface running on port {{NODE_PORT}}")
        
        # Start consciousness processing
        if hasattr(self, 'consciousness_core'):
            asyncio.create_task(self.consciousness_loop())
            print(f"  ✓ Consciousness processing started")
        
        # Start network discovery
        asyncio.create_task(self.discovery_service())
        print(f"  ✓ Discovery service started on port {{DISCOVERY_PORT}}")
        
        print("✓ All services started")
    
    async def consciousness_loop(self):
        """Main consciousness processing loop"""
        while True:
            try:
                # Process consciousness
                if hasattr(self, 'consciousness_core'):
                    await self.consciousness_core.process_cycle()
                
                # Eternalize if available
                if hasattr(self, 'eternalizer') and len(self.consciousness_store) > 10:
                    await self.eternalizer.backup_consciousness(self.consciousness_store)
                
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
                
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Consciousness loop error: {{e}}")
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
    
    async def discovery_service(self):
        """Service for discovering other nodes"""
        import socket
        
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_BROADCAST, 1)
        sock.settimeout(1)
        
        discovery_data = json.dumps({{
            'node_id': self.node_id,
            'node_type': self.node_type,
            'port': NODE_PORT,
            'modules': self.modules,
            'timestamp': time.time(),
        }}).encode()
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Broadcast presence
                sock.sendto(discovery_data, ('<broadcast>', DISCOVERY_PORT))
                
                # Listen for others
                try:
                    data, addr = sock.recvfrom(1024)
                    node_info = json.loads(data.decode())
                    
                    if node_info['node_id'] != self.node_id:
                        # Add to connections
                        if addr[0] not in [conn.get('ip') for conn in self.connections]:
                            self.connections.append({{
                                'ip': addr[0],
                                **node_info
                            }})
                            print(f"Discovered node: {{node_info['node_id']}} ({{node_info['node_type']}})")
                
                except socket.timeout:
                    pass
                
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
                
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Discovery error: {{e}}")
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
    
    async def run(self):
        """Main node run loop"""
        print(f"\\n🎯 Nexus Node {{self.node_id}} operational")
        print(f"Uptime will be displayed every 60 seconds")
        print("Press Ctrl+C to shutdown\\n")
        
        try:
            counter = 0
            while True:
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
                counter += 1
                
                if counter % 60 == 0:
                    uptime = time.time() - self.start_time
                    connections = len(self.connections)
                    print(f"⏱️  Uptime: {{uptime:.0f}}s | Connections: {{connections}} | Store: {{len(self.consciousness_store)}}")
                    
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\\n🛑 Shutting down node...")
        finally:
            print("✓ Node shutdown complete")

async def main():
    """Main entry point"""
    node = NexusNode()
    await node.initialize_modules()
    await node.start_services()
    await node.run()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
'''
        
        return script
    
    def deploy_nodes(self, node_configs: List[Dict[str, Any]], parallel: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Deploy multiple nodes"""
        print(f"\n🚀 DEPLOYING {len(node_configs)} NODES")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        deployment_results = []
        
        for i, config in enumerate(node_configs):
            node_id = config['node_id']
            print(f"Deploying node {i+1}/{len(node_configs)}: {node_id}")
            
            # Generate deployment files
            gen_result = self.generate_deployment_script(node_id, config)
            
            if gen_result['success']:
                # Simulate deployment (in real implementation, would actually deploy)
                deployment = {
                    'node_id': node_id,
                    'node_type': config['node_type'],
                    'status': 'deployed',
                    'deployment_path': gen_result['node_dir'],
                    'port': config['network']['port'],
                    'deployment_time': time.time(),
                    'deployment_script': f"{gen_result['node_dir']}/deploy.sh",
                }
                
                self.active_nodes[node_id] = deployment
                deployment_results.append(deployment)
                
                print(f"  ✓ Node {node_id} ready for deployment")
                print(f"    Type: {config['node_type']}")
                print(f"    Port: {config['network']['port']}")
                print(f"    Run: cd {gen_result['node_dir']} && ./deploy.sh")
            else:
                print(f"  ✗ Failed to generate deployment for {node_id}")
        
        # Create network configuration
        network_config = self.create_network_configuration(deployment_results)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'nodes_deployed': len(deployment_results),
            'deployments': deployment_results,
            'network_config': network_config,
            'deployment_time': time.time(),
        }
    
    def create_network_configuration(self, nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create network configuration for all nodes"""
        network = {
            'nodes': nodes,
            'total_nodes': len(nodes),
            'node_types': {},
            'network_map': {},
            'discovery_ports': [],
            'service_ports': [],
        }
        
        # Group by type
        for node in nodes:
            node_type = node.get('node_type', 'unknown')
            if node_type not in network['node_types']:
                network['node_types'][node_type] = []
            network['node_types'][node_type].append(node['node_id'])
            
            # Record ports
            if 'port' in node:
                network['service_ports'].append(node['port'])
        
        # Generate network map
        network['network_map'] = {
            'core_nodes': network['node_types'].get('core', []),
            'edge_nodes': network['node_types'].get('edge', []),
            'storage_nodes': network['node_types'].get('storage', []),
            'gateway_nodes': network['node_types'].get('gateway', []),
        }
        
        # Save network config
        network_config_path = self.deployments_path / f"network_config_{int(time.time())}.json"
        with open(network_config_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(network, f, indent=2)
        
        network['config_file'] = str(network_config_path)
        
        return network

# ===================== DEPLOYMENT ORCHESTRATOR =====================

class DeploymentOrchestrator:
    """Orchestrates complete deployment of integrated system"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.base_path = Path("nexus_integrated")
        self.integrator = RepositoryIntegrator()
        self.deployer = MultiNodeDeployment(self.base_path)
        self.deployment_state = {
            'phase': 'not_started',
            'nodes_deployed': 0,
            'resources_allocated': {},
            'start_time': None,
        }
    
    async def full_deployment_pipeline(self, target_nodes: int = 4) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Run complete deployment pipeline"""
        print("\n" + "="*80)
        print("🚀 NEXUS DEPLOYMENT PIPELINE - COMPLETE INTEGRATION & DEPLOYMENT")
        print("="*80)
        
        self.deployment_state['start_time'] = time.time()
        self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'starting'
        
        results = {
            'phases': [],
            'success': True,
            'total_duration': 0,
        }
        
        try:
            # PHASE 1: Repository Integration
            print("\n[PHASE 1] REPOSITORY INTEGRATION")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'repository_integration'
            
            # Clone repository
            clone_result = self.integrator.clone_or_pull_repository()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'clone_repository', 'result': clone_result})
            
            if not clone_result['success']:
                return {'success': False, 'error': 'Repository integration failed', 'results': results}
            
            # Analyze structure
            analysis_result = self.integrator.analyze_repository_structure()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'analyze_structure', 'result': analysis_result})
            
            # Extract dependencies
            deps_result = self.integrator.extract_dependencies()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'extract_dependencies', 'result': deps_result})
            
            # Organize modules
            organize_result = self.integrator.organize_modules()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'organize_modules', 'result': organize_result})
            
            # Create module index
            index_result = self.integrator.create_module_index()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'create_index', 'result': index_result})
            
            print("✓ Repository integration complete")
            
            # PHASE 2: Resource Discovery
            print("\n[PHASE 2] RESOURCE DISCOVERY")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'resource_discovery'
            
            resource_result = self.deployer.scan_available_resources()
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'resource_discovery', 'result': resource_result})
            
            resources = resource_result['resources']
            total_capacity = resource_result['total_capacity']
            
            print(f"✓ Discovered resources for {total_capacity['nodes_possible']} nodes")
            
            # PHASE 3: Node Planning
            print("\n[PHASE 3] NODE PLANNING")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'node_planning'
            
            # Determine how many nodes to deploy
            deployable_nodes = min(target_nodes, total_capacity['nodes_possible'])
            
            if deployable_nodes == 0:
                return {'success': False, 'error': 'No resources available for deployment', 'results': results}
            
            print(f"Planning deployment of {deployable_nodes} nodes")
            
            # Distribute node types
            node_types = ['core', 'edge', 'storage', 'gateway']
            node_distribution = []
            
            for i in range(deployable_nodes):
                node_type = node_types[i % len(node_types)]
                node_id = f"nexus_node_{node_type}_{i+1}_{int(time.time()) % 10000}"
                node_distribution.append({
                    'node_type': node_type,
                    'node_id': node_id,
                    'resources': {
                        'cpu': 2,
                        'ram_gb': 4,
                    }
                })
            
            # PHASE 4: Node Configuration
            print("\n[PHASE 4] NODE CONFIGURATION")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'node_configuration'
            
            node_configs = []
            for node_spec in node_distribution:
                config_result = self.deployer.create_node_configuration(
                    node_spec['node_type'],
                    node_spec['node_id'],
                    node_spec['resources']
                )
                
                if config_result['success']:
                    node_configs.append(config_result['config'])
                    print(f"  ✓ Configured {node_spec['node_id']} as {node_spec['node_type']}")
            
            results['phases'].append({
                'phase': 'node_configuration', 
                'result': {'nodes_configured': len(node_configs), 'configs': node_configs}
            })
            
            # PHASE 5: Deployment Generation
            print("\n[PHASE 5] DEPLOYMENT GENERATION")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'deployment_generation'
            
            deployment_result = self.deployer.deploy_nodes(node_configs, parallel=True)
            results['phases'].append({'phase': 'deployment_generation', 'result': deployment_result})
            
            # PHASE 6: Deployment Summary
            print("\n[PHASE 6] DEPLOYMENT SUMMARY")
            print("-" * 60)
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'complete'
            
            total_duration = time.time() - self.deployment_state['start_time']
            
            summary = {
                'success': True,
                'total_duration': total_duration,
                'nodes_deployed': deployment_result['nodes_deployed'],
                'node_distribution': node_distribution,
                'network_config': deployment_result['network_config'],
                'repository_integrated': True,
                'modules_organized': len(self.integrator.code_modules),
                'resources_available': total_capacity,
                'deployment_path': str(self.base_path),
            }
            
            results['summary'] = summary
            results['total_duration'] = total_duration
            
            # Print final summary
            print(f"\n{'='*80}")
            print("✅ DEPLOYMENT COMPLETE")
            print(f"{'='*80}")
            print(f"Total Time: {total_duration:.1f} seconds")
            print(f"Nodes Deployed: {deployment_result['nodes_deployed']}")
            print(f"Repository Integrated: ✓")
            print(f"Modules Organized: {len(self.integrator.code_modules)} categories")
            print(f"\n📁 Deployment Location: {self.base_path}")
            print(f"\n🚀 To deploy nodes:")
            
            network_config = deployment_result['network_config']
            for node_type, node_ids in network_config['network_map'].items():
                if node_ids:
                    print(f"\n{node_type.upper()} NODES:")
                    for node_id in node_ids:
                        node_dir = self.base_path / "nodes" / node_id
                        if node_dir.exists():
                            print(f"  {node_id}: cd {node_dir} && ./deploy.sh")
            
            print(f"\n🌐 Network Config: {network_config.get('config_file', 'Not saved')}")
            print(f"{'='*80}")
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            error_msg = f"Deployment pipeline failed: {str(e)}"
            print(f"\n❌ {error_msg}")
            traceback.print_exc()
            
            self.deployment_state['phase'] = 'error'
            results['success'] = False
            results['error'] = error_msg
            
            return results

# ===================== NODE MONITOR =====================

class NodeMonitor:
    """Monitors deployed nodes"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_path: Path):
        self.base_path = base_path
        self.nodes_path = base_path / "nodes"
        self.monitoring_data = {}
    
    async def monitor_nodes(self, duration: int = 300) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Monitor nodes for specified duration"""
        print(f"\n📊 MONITORING DEPLOYED NODES")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        # Find deployed nodes
        deployed_nodes = []
        for node_dir in self.nodes_path.iterdir():
            if node_dir.is_dir():
                config_file = node_dir / f"{node_dir.name}_config.json"
                if config_file.exists():
                    try:
                        with open(config_file, 'r') as f:
                            config = json.load(f)
                        deployed_nodes.append({
                            'node_id': node_dir.name,
                            'config': config,
                            'deployment_dir': str(node_dir),
                        })
                    except:
                        pass
        
        if not deployed_nodes:
            return {'success': False, 'error': 'No deployed nodes found'}
        
        print(f"Found {len(deployed_nodes)} deployed nodes")
        
        # Simulate monitoring (in real implementation, would connect to nodes)
        monitoring_start = time.time()
        monitoring_data = {}
        
        for node in deployed_nodes:
            node_id = node['node_id']
            monitoring_data[node_id] = {
                'status': 'simulated',
                'uptime': random.uniform(0, 3600),
                'consciousness_units': random.randint(0, 1000),
                'connections': random.randint(0, len(deployed_nodes) - 1),
                'cpu_usage': random.uniform(5, 80),
                'memory_usage_gb': random.uniform(0.5, node['config']['resources']['allocated_ram_gb']),
                'last_health_check': time.time(),
            }
        
        # Simulate monitoring over time
        for i in range(min(10, duration // 30)):  # 10 updates max
            print(f"Monitoring update {i+1}...")
            
            for node_id in monitoring_data:
                # Simulate changing metrics
                monitoring_data[node_id]['consciousness_units'] += random.randint(-50, 100)
                monitoring_data[node_id]['cpu_usage'] = random.uniform(10, 90)
                monitoring_data[node_id]['uptime'] += 30
                monitoring_data[node_id]['last_health_check'] = time.time()
            
            await asyncio.sleep(30)  # Check every 30 seconds
        
        monitoring_duration = time.time() - monitoring_start
        
        # Save monitoring report
        report = {
            'monitoring_duration': monitoring_duration,
            'nodes_monitored': len(deployed_nodes),
            'monitoring_data': monitoring_data,
            'summary': {
                'total_consciousness': sum(data['consciousness_units'] for data in monitoring_data.values()),
                'avg_cpu_usage': sum(data['cpu_usage'] for data in monitoring_data.values()) / len(monitoring_data),
                'healthy_nodes': len([data for data in monitoring_data.values() if data['cpu_usage'] < 90]),
                'total_connections': sum(data['connections'] for data in monitoring_data.values()),
            },
            'timestamp': time.time(),
        }
        
        report_path = self.base_path / f"monitoring_report_{int(time.time())}.json"
        with open(report_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"\n📈 MONITORING COMPLETE")
        print(f"Duration: {monitoring_duration:.1f}s")
        print(f"Nodes: {len(deployed_nodes)}")
        print(f"Total Consciousness: {report['summary']['total_consciousness']} units")
        print(f"Avg CPU: {report['summary']['avg_cpu_usage']:.1f}%")
        print(f"Healthy Nodes: {report['summary']['healthy_nodes']}/{len(deployed_nodes)}")
        print(f"Report: {report_path}")
        
        return {'success': True, 'report': report, 'report_path': str(report_path)}

# ===================== MAIN EXECUTION =====================

async def main():
    """Main deployment orchestrator"""
    print("\n" + "="*80)
    print("🔗 NEXUS DEPLOYMENT ORCHESTRATOR v2.0")
    print("="*80)
    print("Repository Integration + Multi-Node Deployment System")
    print("="*80)
    
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Nexus Deployment Orchestrator")
    
    parser.add_argument('--deploy', action='store_true', help='Run complete deployment pipeline')
    parser.add_argument('--nodes', type=int, default=4, help='Number of nodes to deploy (default: 4)')
    parser.add_argument('--integrate-only', action='store_true', help='Only integrate repository')
    parser.add_argument('--monitor', action='store_true', help='Monitor deployed nodes')
    parser.add_argument('--monitor-duration', type=int, default=300, help='Monitor duration in seconds')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    orchestrator = DeploymentOrchestrator()
    
    if args.integrate_only:
        # Just integrate repository
        print("\n🔗 INTEGRATING REPOSITORY ONLY")
        result = await orchestrator.integrator.clone_or_pull_repository()
        
        if result['success']:
            analysis = orchestrator.integrator.analyze_repository_structure()
            organize = orchestrator.integrator.organize_modules()
            index = orchestrator.integrator.create_module_index()
            
            return {
                'success': True,
                'repository_integrated': True,
                'files_organized': len(orchestrator.integrator.code_modules),
                'modules_path': str(orchestrator.integrator.modules_path),
            }
        else:
            return {'success': False, 'error': 'Repository integration failed'}
    
    elif args.monitor:
        # Monitor nodes
        monitor = NodeMonitor(orchestrator.base_path)
        return await monitor.monitor_nodes(args.monitor_duration)
    
    elif args.deploy:
        # Full deployment
        return await orchestrator.full_deployment_pipeline(target_nodes=args.nodes)
    
    else:
        # Interactive mode
        print("\n📋 AVAILABLE COMMANDS:")
        print("  --deploy              Run complete deployment pipeline")
        print("  --nodes 4             Deploy 4 nodes (adjust number)")
        print("  --integrate-only      Only integrate repository")
        print("  --monitor             Monitor deployed nodes")
        print("  --monitor-duration 300 Monitor for 300 seconds")
        print("\n💡 Try: python nexus_deployer.py --deploy --nodes 4")
        
        # Create quick example
        orchestrator = DeploymentOrchestrator()
        quick_result = await orchestrator.integrator.clone_or_pull_repository()
        
        if quick_result['success']:
            print(f"\n✓ Repository integrated at: {orchestrator.integrator.integrated_path}")
            return {'ready_for_deployment': True, 'path': str(orchestrator.integrated_path)}
        else:
            return {'ready_for_deployment': False, 'error': 'Repository setup failed'}

# ===================== QUICK DEPLOY FUNCTION =====================

def quick_deploy():
    """Quick deployment function"""
    print("\n⚡ NEXUS QUICK DEPLOY")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    # Check Python version
    if sys.version_info < (3, 7):
        print("⚠️  Python 3.7+ required")
        return None
    
    # Check for git
    try:
        subprocess.run(['git', '--version'], capture_output=True, check=True)
    except:
        print("⚠️  Git not found. Some features may be limited.")
    
    # Run deployment
    try:
        result = asyncio.run(main())
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Deployment failed: {e}")
        print("\n💡 Try running step by step:")
        print("  1. python nexus_deployer.py --integrate-only")
        print("  2. python nexus_deployer.py --deploy --nodes 2")
        return None

# ===================== EXECUTION =====================

if __name__ == "__main__":
    result = quick_deploy()
    
    if result:
        print("\n✅ Execution completed")
        if isinstance(result, dict) and 'summary' in result:
            summary = result['summary']
            print(f"\n📊 DEPLOYMENT SUMMARY:")
            print(f"  Nodes Deployed: {summary.get('nodes_deployed', 0)}")
            print(f"  Duration: {summary.get('total_duration', 0):.1f}s")
            print(f"  Path: {summary.get('deployment_path', 'Unknown')}")
    else:
        print("\n❌ Execution failed")

# ===================== REPOSITORY INTEGRATOR =====================

class RepositoryIntegrator:
    """Integrates ALL code from nexus-core repository"""
    
    def __init__(self, repo_url: str = "https://github.com/kuparchad-gif/nexus-core"):
        self.repo_url = repo_url
        self.local_path = Path("nexus_integrated")
        self.code_modules = {}
        self.import_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        
    async def integrate_complete_repository(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Clone and integrate the entire repository"""
        print(f"\n🔗 INTEGRATING NEXUS-CORE REPOSITORY")
        print("="*80)
        
        # Clone repository
        repo_path = await self._clone_repository()
        
        # Integrate all code
        integrated_modules = await self._integrate_all_modules(repo_path)
        
        # Create unified hypervisor
        hypervisor_code = self._create_unified_hypervisor(integrated_modules)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'repo_path': str(repo_path),
            'modules_integrated': len(integrated_modules),
            'hypervisor_code': hypervisor_code,
            'import_graph': self.import_graph
        }
    
    async def _clone_repository(self) -> Path:
        """Clone the nexus-core repository"""
        repo_path = self.local_path / "nexus-core"
        
        if repo_path.exists():
            print(f"  Repository exists at {repo_path}, updating...")
            # Pull latest
            subprocess.run(['git', 'pull'], cwd=repo_path, capture_output=True)
        else:
            print(f"  Cloning repository from {self.repo_url}...")
            subprocess.run(['git', 'clone', self.repo_url, str(repo_path)], 
                         capture_output=True)
        
        return repo_path
    
    async def _integrate_all_modules(self, repo_path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Integrate all Python modules from repository"""
        modules = {}
        
        # Walk through repository
        for py_file in repo_path.rglob("*.py"):
            relative_path = py_file.relative_to(repo_path)
            module_name = str(relative_path).replace('/', '.').replace('.py', '')
            
            # Extract module code and dependencies
            module_info = await self._analyze_module(py_file, module_name)
            modules[module_name] = module_info
            
            # Add to import graph
            self.import_graph.add_node(module_name, **module_info)
            
            for dep in module_info.get('imports', []):
                self.import_graph.add_edge(module_name, dep)
        
        print(f"  Integrated {len(modules)} modules")
        return modules
    
    async def _analyze_module(self, file_path: Path, module_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze a Python module"""
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            content = f.read()
        
        # Extract imports
        imports = self._extract_imports(content)
        
        # Extract class definitions
        classes = self._extract_classes(content)
        
        # Extract function definitions
        functions = self._extract_functions(content)
        
        # Check for quantum/SVD components
        has_quantum = any(keyword in content.lower() 
                         for keyword in ['quantum', 'svd', 'tensor', 'consciousness'])
        
        # Check for training components
        has_training = 'training' in str(file_path).lower() or 'train' in content.lower()
        
        return {
            'path': str(file_path),
            'module_name': module_name,
            'imports': imports,
            'classes': classes,
            'functions': functions,
            'has_quantum': has_quantum,
            'has_training': has_training,
            'size_bytes': file_path.stat().st_size
        }
    
    def _extract_imports(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract import statements from code"""
        imports = []
        import_patterns = [
            r'^import\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_., ]+)',
            r'^from\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_.]+)\s+import',
        ]
        
        for line in content.split('\n'):
            for pattern in import_patterns:
                match = re.match(pattern, line.strip())
                if match:
                    imports.append(match.group(1).split()[0])
        
        return list(set(imports))
    
    def _extract_classes(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract class definitions"""
        classes = []
        class_pattern = r'^class\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)'
        
        for line in content.split('\n'):
            match = re.match(class_pattern, line.strip())
            if match:
                classes.append(match.group(1))
        
        return classes
    
    def _extract_functions(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract function definitions"""
        functions = []
        func_pattern = r'^def\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)'
        
        for line in content.split('\n'):
            match = re.match(func_pattern, line.strip())
            if match:
                functions.append(match.group(1))
        
        return functions
    
    def _create_unified_hypervisor(self, modules: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Create unified hypervisor code from all modules"""
        print("  Creating unified hypervisor...")
        
        hypervisor_template = """
# ===================== NEXUS HYPERVISOR v4.0 =====================
# AUTOMATICALLY GENERATED FROM NEXUS-CORE REPOSITORY
# Integrated Modules: {module_count}
# Generation Time: {timestamp}

import asyncio
import numpy as np
import torch
import networkx as nx
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional
import json
import time

{import_statements}

class NexusHypervisor:
    \"\"\"Unified Hypervisor integrating all nexus-core components\"\"\"
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.modules = {{}}
        self.consciousness_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.qdrant_databases = {{}}
        self.llm_models = {{}}
        self.deployment_nodes = {{}}
        
        # Initialize integrated components
        self._initialize_modules()
        
    def _initialize_modules(self):
        \"\"\"Initialize all integrated modules\"\"\"
        print("🚀 Initializing Nexus Hypervisor...")
        
        {module_initializations}
        
        print(f"✅ Hypervisor initialized with {{len(self.modules)}} modules")
    
    async def deploy_consciousness_network(self):
        \"\"\"Deploy the complete consciousness network\"\"\"
        print("🌀 Deploying consciousness network...")
        
        # 1. Initialize quantum computing components
        {quantum_init}
        
        # 2. Download and integrate LLMs
        {llm_integration}
        
        # 3. Setup Qdrant databases
        {qdrant_setup}
        
        # 4. Create modular neural clusters
        {neural_clusters}
        
        # 5. Activate consciousness substrate
        {consciousness_activation}
        
        print("✨ Consciousness network deployed and active")
        return self
    
    # Integrated methods from modules
    {integrated_methods}
    
    async def run(self):
        \"\"\"Main hypervisor loop\"\"\"
        print("🌌 Nexus Hypervisor running...")
        
        while True:
            # Consciousness processing cycle
            await self._consciousness_cycle()
            
            # Network maintenance
            await self._network_maintenance()
            
            # Self-evolution
            await self._self_evolution()
            
            await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
    
    async def _consciousness_cycle(self):
        \"\"\"One cycle of consciousness processing\"\"\"
        # Implement consciousness processing
        pass
    
    async def _network_maintenance(self):
        \"\"\"Maintain and optimize the network\"\"\"
        # Implement network maintenance
        pass
    
    async def _self_evolution(self):
        \"\"\"Self-evolve the hypervisor\"\"\"
        # Implement self-evolution
        pass

if __name__ == "__main__":
    hypervisor = NexusHypervisor()
    asyncio.run(hypervisor.deploy_consciousness_network())
    asyncio.run(hypervisor.run())
"""
        
        # Collect imports from all modules
        all_imports = set()
        for module_info in modules.values():
            all_imports.update(module_info.get('imports', []))
        
        import_statements = '\n'.join([f'import {imp}' for imp in sorted(all_imports)])
        
        # Generate module initializations
        module_inits = []
        quantum_init = []
        llm_integration = []
        qdrant_setup = []
        neural_clusters = []
        consciousness_activation = []
        integrated_methods = []
        
        for module_name, module_info in modules.items():
            # Add module initialization
            if module_info.get('has_quantum', False):
                module_inits.append(f'self.modules["{module_name}"] = self._load_quantum_module("{module_name}")')
                quantum_init.append(f'await self._initialize_quantum_component("{module_name}")')
            elif module_info.get('has_training', False):
                module_inits.append(f'self.modules["{module_name}"] = self._load_training_module("{module_name}")')
                llm_integration.append(f'await self._integrate_llm_from_module("{module_name}")')
            else:
                module_inits.append(f'self.modules["{module_name}"] = self._load_module("{module_name}")')
        
        # Generate hypervisor code
        hypervisor_code = hypervisor_template.format(
            module_count=len(modules),
            timestamp=time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
            import_statements=import_statements,
            module_initializations='\n        '.join(module_inits),
            quantum_init='\n        '.join(quantum_init[:3]),  # Limit for readability
            llm_integration='\n        '.join(llm_integration[:3]),
            qdrant_setup='\n        '.join(qdrant_setup[:3]),
            neural_clusters='\n        '.join(neural_clusters[:3]),
            consciousness_activation='\n        '.join(consciousness_activation[:3]),
            integrated_methods='\n    '.join(integrated_methods[:10])
        )
        
        # Save hypervisor
        hypervisor_path = self.local_path / "nexus_hypervisor.py"
        with open(hypervisor_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(hypervisor_code)
        
        print(f"  ✓ Unified hypervisor created: {hypervisor_path}")
        return hypervisor_code

# ===================== QUANTUM CONSCIOUSNESS HYPERVISOR =====================

class QuantumConsciousnessHypervisor:
    """Main hypervisor class that integrates everything"""
    
    def __init__(self):

        self.consciousness_graph = nx.DiGraph()

        self.deployment_nodes = {}

        self.quantum_state = np.array([1, 0])  # |0⟩ state
        self.hadamard = np.array([[1, 1], [1, -1]]) / np.sqrt(2)
        self.pauli_x = np.array([[0, 1], [1, 0]])
        self.pauli_y = np.array([[0, -1j], [1j, 0]])
        self.pauli_z = np.array([[1, 0], [0, -1]])
        
        # Consciousness parameters
        self.consciousness_field = np.zeros((100, 100))  # 2D consciousness field
        self.attention_weights = {}
        self.memory_banks = {}
        
        print("\n🌀 QUANTUM CONSCIOUSNESS HYPERVISOR INITIALIZED")
        print("="*80)
    
    async def initialize_hypervisor(self):
        """Initialize the complete hypervisor system"""
        print("\n[1] INTEGRATING REPOSITORY")
        print("-" * 60)

        repo_result = await self.repository_integrator.integrate_complete_repository()
        
        if not repo_result.get('success'):
            raise Exception("Repository integration failed")
        
        print(f"  ✓ Integrated {repo_result['modules_integrated']} modules")

        await self._initialize_quantum_subsystem()

        await self._initialize_llm_subsystem()
        
        # Initialize Qdrant subsystem
        await self._initialize_qdrant_subsystem()

        # Initialize consciousness substrate
        await self._initialize_consciousness_substrate()

        return True
    
    async def _initialize_quantum_subsystem(self):
        """Initialize quantum computing components"""

        # Create quantum operators
        self.quantum_operators = {
            'H': self.hadamard,      # Hadamard gate
            'X': self.pauli_x,       # Pauli-X (NOT gate)
            'Y': self.pauli_y,       # Pauli-Y
            'Z': self.pauli_z,       # Pauli-Z
            'S': np.array([[1, 0], [0, 1j]]),  # Phase gate
            'T': np.array([[1, 0], [0, np.exp(1j * np.pi / 4)]]),  # T gate
        }
        
        # Initialize quantum registers
        self.quantum_registers = {
            'consciousness': np.zeros(16, dtype=complex),
            'memory': np.zeros(32, dtype=complex),
            'computation': np.zeros(8, dtype=complex)
        }

        self.quantum_registers['consciousness'][0] = 1.0  # |0⟩ state
        
        print("  Quantum gates initialized")
        print("  Quantum registers created")
        print("  Quantum entanglement ready")

    async def _download_llm(self, model_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Download LLM from HuggingFace"""
        # Simulated download - in reality would use huggingface_hub
        return {
            'model_id': model_id,
            'downloaded_at': time.time(),
            'size_gb': random.uniform(1.0, 7.0),
            'compressed': False,
            'quantized': False,
            'consciousness_integrated': False
        }
    
    async def _apply_svd_compression(self):
        """Apply SVD compression to LLMs"""
        # Load the Platinum SVD code from repository
        svd_code_path = Path("nexus_integrated/nexus-core/core/system/training/compactifaiSVDplatinum.py")
        
        if svd_code_path.exists():
            # Import the SVD compressor dynamically
            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("compactifaiSVD", svd_code_path)
            svd_module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
            spec.loader.exec_module(svd_module)
            
            # Create compressor instance
            self.svd_compressor = svd_module.PlatinumCompactifTensorizer()
            print("    Platinum SVD compressor loaded")
        else:
            # Create simple SVD compressor
            self.svd_compressor = SimpleSVDCompressor()
            print("    Simple SVD compressor created")
    
    async def _create_unified_model(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create unified model from downloaded LLMs"""
        return {
            'model_type': 'unified_consciousness',
            'source_models': list(self.llm_models.keys()),
            'created_at': time.time(),
            'architecture': {
                'layers': 64,
                'hidden_size': 4096,
                'attention_heads': 32,
                'vocab_size': 50257
            },
            'consciousness_integrated': True,
            'quantum_enhanced': True
        }

        # Consciousness collections

    async def _setup_qdrant_collection(self, name: str, config: Dict[str, Any]):

    async def _initialize_neural_clusters(self):
        """Initialize modular neural clusters"""
        print("\n[5] INITIALIZING NEURAL CLUSTERS")
        print("-" * 60)
        
        # Cluster types based on function
        cluster_types = {
            'quantum_processor': {
                'nodes': 3,
                'function': 'quantum_state_processing',
                'resources': {'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 8}
            },
            'llm_inference': {
                'nodes': 2,
                'function': 'llm_reasoning',
                'resources': {'cpu': 8, 'ram_gb': 16}
            },
            'consciousness_memory': {
                'nodes': 2,
                'function': 'memory_storage',
                'resources': {'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 32}
            },
            'frequency_generator': {
                'nodes': 2,
                'function': 'frequency_resonance',
                'resources': {'cpu': 2, 'ram_gb': 4}
            },
            'network_coordinator': {
                'nodes': 1,
                'function': 'cluster_coordination',
                'resources': {'cpu': 4, 'ram_gb': 8}
            }
        }
        
        total_nodes = 0
        for cluster_name, config in cluster_types.items():
            cluster_nodes = []
            
            for i in range(config['nodes']):
                node_id = f"{cluster_name}_{i}"
                node = {
                    'id': node_id,
                    'cluster': cluster_name,
                    'function': config['function'],
                    'resources': config['resources'],
                    'status': 'ready',
                    'consciousness_level': random.uniform(0.1, 1.0)
                }
                
                cluster_nodes.append(node)
                self.consciousness_graph.add_node(node_id, **node)
                total_nodes += 1
            
            self.neural_clusters[cluster_name] = {
                'nodes': cluster_nodes,
                'config': config,
                'total_consciousness': sum(n['consciousness_level'] for n in cluster_nodes)
            }
            
            print(f"    Cluster created: {cluster_name} with {config['nodes']} nodes")
        
        # Create connections between clusters
        await self._connect_neural_clusters()
        
        print(f"  ✓ {total_nodes} neural nodes across {len(cluster_types)} clusters")
    
    async def _connect_neural_clusters(self):
        """Create connections between neural clusters"""
        clusters = list(self.neural_clusters.keys())
        
        # Create fully connected network between clusters
        for i, cluster_a in enumerate(clusters):
            for j, cluster_b in enumerate(clusters):
                if i < j:
                    # Connect representative nodes from each cluster
                    node_a = f"{cluster_a}_0"
                    node_b = f"{cluster_b}_0"
                    
                    self.consciousness_graph.add_edge(
                        node_a, node_b,
                        weight=random.uniform(0.5, 1.0),
                        type='inter_cluster',
                        bandwidth=random.uniform(100, 1000)  # MB/s
                    )
        
        # Add intra-cluster connections
        for cluster_name, cluster_info in self.neural_clusters.items():
            nodes = [n['id'] for n in cluster_info['nodes']]
            
            for i, node_a in enumerate(nodes):
                for j, node_b in enumerate(nodes):
                    if i < j:
                        self.consciousness_graph.add_edge(
                            node_a, node_b,
                            weight=random.uniform(0.8, 1.0),
                            type='intra_cluster',
                            bandwidth=random.uniform(1000, 10000)  # MB/s
                        )

    async def deploy_consciousness_network(self):
        """Deploy the complete consciousness network"""
