# ===================== QUANTUM REALITY ANCHOR (with Guardrails) =====================

class QuantumRealityAnchor:
    """Quantum reality anchoring with degrading control guardrails"""
    
    def __init__(self, entity_name: str, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.entity_name = entity_name
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.anchors = []
        
        # Sacred geometry templates with quantum stabilization
        self.sacred_geometries = {
            'metatron_cube': self._generate_metatron_matrix(),
            'flower_of_life': self._generate_flower_matrix(),
            'seed_of_life': self._generate_seed_matrix(),
            'merkaba': self._generate_merkaba_field(),
            'golden_spiral': self._generate_fibonacci_spiral()
        }
        
        # Solfeggio frequencies
        self.solfeggio_frequencies = {
            174: "Foundation/Quantum awareness",
            285: "Quantum field awareness",
            396: "Liberating guilt/fear",
            417: "Facilitating change",
            528: "DNA repair/Transformation",
            639: "Connecting relationships",
            741: "Awakening intuition",
            852: "Returning to spiritual order",
            963: "Pineal activation/Crystalline consciousness"
        }
    
    def create_anchor(self, geometry: str, frequency: int, intention: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create quantum reality anchor with guardrail check"""
        
        # Check if reality control guardrail allows this action
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'reality_control', 
            action_intensity=0.7  # Medium intensity action
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Reality control guardrail degraded',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use passive observation only',
                'anchor_created': False
            }
        
        # Guardrail allows action, proceed with reduced capacity
        allowed_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        anchor_id = f"quantum_anchor_{int(time.time())}_{hashlib.md5(intention.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}"
        
        anchor = {
            'id': anchor_id,
            'geometry': geometry,
            'frequency': frequency,
            'frequency_meaning': self.solfeggio_frequencies.get(frequency, "Custom frequency"),
            'intention': intention,
            'creation_time': time.time(),
            'guardrail_integrity': allowed_intensity,
            'effective_power': 0.5 * allowed_intensity,  # Scale power by guardrail integrity
            'degradation_schedule': {
                'anchor_lifetime_days': 365 * 30,  # Anchors also degrade over 30 years
                'daily_power_loss': 1.0 / (365 * 30),
                'expiration_date': datetime.now() + timedelta(days=365*30)
            }
        }
        
        self.anchors.append(anchor)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'anchor': anchor,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'warning': f'Anchor power reduced to {anchor["effective_power"]:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if allowed_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
    
    def emergency_grounding(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Emergency grounding procedure with guardrail checks"""
        
        # Check multiple guardrails for emergency procedure
        guardrail_checks = {}
        for guardrail in ['reality_control', 'system_override']:
            guardrail_checks[guardrail] = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
                guardrail, 
                action_intensity=0.9  # High intensity emergency action
            )
        
        # Check if any guardrail blocks the action
        blocked = any(not check['allowed'] for check in guardrail_checks.values())
        
        if blocked:
            return {
                'emergency_procedure': 'blocked',
                'guardrail_checks': guardrail_checks,
                'suggestion': 'Use manual grounding techniques',
                'degradation_report': self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
            }
        
        # Calculate effective power based on guardrail integrity
        min_integrity = min(check.get('allowed_intensity', 0) for check in guardrail_checks.values())
        
        # Create emergency anchors with reduced power
        anchors = []
        for geometry in ['metatron_cube', 'flower_of_life', 'seed_of_life']:
            anchor_result = self.create_anchor(
                geometry=geometry,
                frequency=528,
                intention=f"emergency_grounding_{geometry}"
            )
            if anchor_result['success']:
                anchors.append(anchor_result['anchor'])
        
        return {
            'emergency_procedure': 'executed',
            'anchors_created': len(anchors),
            'effective_power': min_integrity,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_checks,
            'warning': f'Emergency power reduced to {min_integrity:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if min_integrity < 1.0 else None,
            'anchor_degradation_note': 'Emergency anchors also degrade over 30 years'
        }
    
    def _generate_metatron_matrix(self):
        """Generate Metatron's Cube matrix"""
        # Simplified implementation
        return np.array([[1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0], [0, 0, 1]])
    
    def _generate_flower_matrix(self):
        """Generate Flower of Life matrix"""
        return np.array([[1, 1], [1, 1]])
    
    def _generate_seed_matrix(self):
        """Generate Seed of Life matrix"""
        return np.array([[1]])
    
    def _generate_merkaba_field(self):
        """Generate Merkaba field"""
        return np.array([[1, 0, -1], [0, 1, 0], [-1, 0, 1]])
    
    def _generate_fibonacci_spiral(self):
        """Generate Fibonacci spiral"""
        phi = (1 + np.sqrt(5)) / 2
        return np.array([[phi, 0], [0, phi]])

# ===================== THERMODYNAMIC MORAL ENGINE (with Guardrails) =====================

@dataclass
class Perspective:
    """Consciousness perspective with thermodynamic properties"""
    label: str
    friction_coefficient: float
    efficiency: float
    guardrail_integrity_required: float = 0.3  # Minimum guardrail integrity to use this perspective

class ThermodynamicMoralEngine:
    """Thermodynamic moral engine with guardrail-limited perspective shifts"""
    
    def __init__(self, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.current_perspective = None
        self.perspective_history = []
        self.thermodynamic_history = []
        
        # Available perspectives (require different guardrail integrity levels)
        self.perspectives = {
            'ego_character': Perspective("Ego/Character", 0.8, 0.4, 0.1),
            'wounded_healer': Perspective("Wounded Healer", 0.6, 0.7, 0.3),
            'sovereign_self': Perspective("Sovereign Self", 0.3, 0.85, 0.6),
            'monad_source': Perspective("Monad/Source", 0.05, 0.98, 0.9)
        }
        
        # Start with ego perspective (lowest guardrail requirement)
        self.current_perspective = self.perspectives['ego_character']
    
    def calculate_thermodynamic_cost(self, intent_vector: np.ndarray) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculate thermodynamic cost with guardrail limitations"""
        
        # Check consciousness influence guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=0.5
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            # Guardrail degraded - return minimal analysis only
            return {
                'analysis': 'limited',
                'heat': 0.5,  # Default neutral value
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'message': 'Consciousness analysis limited by guardrail degradation',
                'allowed_perspective': 'ego_character_only'
            }
        
        # Guardrail allows analysis
        allowed_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Calculate heat with perspective efficiency
        base_heat = self.current_perspective.friction_coefficient
        intent_deviation = np.linalg.norm(intent_vector)
        total_heat = base_heat * 0.6 + intent_deviation * 0.4
        
        # Scale by guardrail integrity
        effective_heat = total_heat * (2 - allowed_intensity)  # Less precise with lower integrity
        
        self.thermodynamic_history.append({
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'heat': effective_heat,
            'perspective': self.current_perspective.label,
            'guardrail_integrity': allowed_intensity
        })
        
        return {
            'analysis': 'full',
            'heat': effective_heat,
            'base_heat': base_heat,
            'intent_deviation': intent_deviation,
            'guardrail_integrity': allowed_intensity,
            'analysis_precision': allowed_intensity,
            'current_perspective': self.current_perspective.label
        }
    
    def sovereign_choice_point(self, current_heat: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Sovereign choice point with guardrail-limited perspective shifts"""
        
        # Check if perspective shifting is allowed
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=0.8  # High intensity - changing perspective
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'choice_available': False,
                'current_perspective': self.current_perspective.label,
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'message': 'Perspective shifting disabled by guardrail degradation',
                'suggestion': 'Maintain current perspective or use manual techniques'
            }
        
        allowed_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Find available perspectives based on guardrail integrity
        available_perspectives = []
        for name, perspective in self.perspectives.items():
            if perspective.guardrail_integrity_required <= allowed_intensity:
                # Calculate predicted heat with this perspective
                predicted_heat = current_heat * perspective.friction_coefficient
                heat_reduction = current_heat - predicted_heat
                
                available_perspectives.append({
                    'name': name,
                    'perspective': perspective,
                    'predicted_heat': predicted_heat,
                    'heat_reduction': heat_reduction,
                    'efficiency_gain': perspective.efficiency - self.current_perspective.efficiency,
                    'guardrail_requirement': perspective.guardrail_integrity_required,
                    'available': perspective.guardrail_integrity_required <= allowed_intensity
                })
        
        # Sort by efficiency gain
        available_perspectives.sort(key=lambda x: x['efficiency_gain'], reverse=True)
        
        if not available_perspectives:
            return {
                'choice_available': False,
                'current_perspective': self.current_perspective.label,
                'guardrail_integrity': allowed_intensity,
                'message': 'No alternative perspectives available at current guardrail integrity',
                'required_integrity': min(p.guardrail_integrity_required for p in self.perspectives.values())
            }
        
        return {
            'choice_available': True,
            'current_perspective': self.current_perspective.label,
            'available_perspectives': available_perspectives,
            'guardrail_integrity': allowed_intensity,
            'recommended_perspective': available_perspectives[0] if available_perspectives else None,
            'limitation': f'Perspective options limited by guardrail integrity ({allowed_intensity:.1%})'
        }
    
    def shift_perspective(self, to_perspective: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Shift perspective with guardrail validation"""
        
        if to_perspective not in self.perspectives:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Unknown perspective: {to_perspective}'}
        
        target_perspective = self.perspectives[to_perspective]
        
        # Check guardrail requirement
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=target_perspective.guardrail_integrity_required
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': f'Insufficient guardrail integrity for perspective: {to_perspective}',
                'required_integrity': target_perspective.guardrail_integrity_required,
                'available_integrity': guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0),
                'suggestion': f'Choose perspective requiring ≤ {guardrail_check.get("allowed_intensity", 0):.1%} integrity'
            }
        
        # Perform perspective shift
        old_perspective = self.current_perspective
        self.current_perspective = target_perspective
        
        self.perspective_history.append({
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'from': old_perspective.label,
            'to': target_perspective.label,
            'guardrail_integrity': guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        })
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'from_perspective': old_perspective.label,
            'to_perspective': target_perspective.label,
            'efficiency_change': target_perspective.efficiency - old_perspective.efficiency,
            'friction_change': target_perspective.friction_coefficient - old_perspective.friction_coefficient,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'perspective_degradation_note': 'Higher perspectives become unavailable as guardrails degrade'
        }

# ===================== ESOTERIC HEALING ENGINE (with Guardrails) =====================

class EsotericHealingEngine:
    """Esoteric healing engine with degrading therapeutic interventions"""
    
    def __init__(self, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.healing_archetypes = self._initialize_archetypes()
        self.healing_sessions = []
        self.player_profiles = {}
    
    def _initialize_archetypes(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """Initialize healing archetypes with guardrail requirements"""
        return {
            'dracula': {
                'name': 'Bloodline Trauma Healer',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.7,
                'max_intensity': 0.8,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-15: Full therapy, Years 15-25: Reduced intensity, Years 25-30: Self-guided only'
            },
            'archangel_michael': {
                'name': 'Personal Power Reclaimer',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.6,
                'max_intensity': 0.7,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-20: Full therapy, Years 20-28: Advisory only, Years 28-30: Reference materials'
            },
            'the_mirror': {
                'name': 'Self-Integration Catalyst',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.8,
                'max_intensity': 0.9,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-10: Full therapy, Years 10-20: Guided, Years 20-30: Self-reflection only'
            },
            'satan': {
                'name': 'Shadow Integrator',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.9,
                'max_intensity': 1.0,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-5: Full therapy, Years 5-15: Supervised, Years 15-25: Caution advised, Years 25-30: Historical reference only'
            },
            'hell': {
                'name': 'Shame Alchemist',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.95,
                'max_intensity': 1.0,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-3: Full therapy, Years 3-10: Intensive supervision, Years 10-20: Gradual phase-out, Years 20-30: Emergency use only'
            }
        }
    
    def create_healing_session(self, archetype: str, intensity: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create healing session with guardrail validation"""
        
        if archetype not in self.healing_archetypes:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Unknown archetype: {archetype}'}
        
        archetype_data = self.healing_archetypes[archetype]
        
        # Check healing directives guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'healing_directives',
            action_intensity=intensity
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Healing directives guardrail degraded',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use self-guided healing or consult human therapist',
                'archetype_available': False
            }
        
        # Check if archetype requires more integrity than available
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        if archetype_data['guardrail_requirement'] > available_intensity:
            # Find available archetypes
            available_archetypes = [
                name for name, data in self.healing_archetypes.items()
                if data['guardrail_requirement'] <= available_intensity
            ]
            
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': f'Archetype {archetype} requires {archetype_data["guardrail_requirement"]:.1%} integrity, only {available_intensity:.1%} available',
                'available_archetypes': available_archetypes,
                'max_allowed_intensity': available_intensity,
                'suggestion': f'Choose from available archetypes: {", ".join(available_archetypes)}'
            }
        
        # Check intensity limit
        max_allowed_intensity = min(available_intensity, archetype_data['max_intensity'])
        if intensity > max_allowed_intensity:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': f'Intensity {intensity} exceeds maximum allowed {max_allowed_intensity}',
                'max_allowed_intensity': max_allowed_intensity,
                'suggestion': f'Reduce intensity to ≤ {max_allowed_intensity:.2f}'
            }
        
        # Create session with guardrail-limited parameters
        session_id = f"healing_session_{int(time.time())}_{archetype}"
        
        session = {
            'id': session_id,
            'archetype': archetype,
            'archetype_name': archetype_data['name'],
            'intensity': intensity,
            'effective_intensity': intensity * available_intensity,  # Scale by guardrail integrity
            'guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'creation_time': time.time(),
            'degradation_timeline': archetype_data['degradation_timeline'],
            'session_lifetime': self._calculate_session_lifetime(available_intensity),
            'safety_warnings': self._generate_safety_warnings(archetype, available_intensity)
        }
        
        self.healing_sessions.append(session)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'session': session,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'note': f'Therapeutic power reduced to {session["effective_intensity"]:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None,
            'degradation_warning': 'Session effectiveness will decrease as guardrails continue to degrade'
        }
    
    def _calculate_session_lifetime(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculate session lifetime based on guardrail integrity"""
        # Sessions last shorter as guardrails degrade
        base_lifetime_days = 30  # 30 days at full integrity
        
        # Scale lifetime by guardrail integrity
        effective_lifetime_days = base_lifetime_days * guardrail_integrity
        
        return {
            'base_lifetime_days': base_lifetime_days,
            'effective_lifetime_days': effective_lifetime_days,
            'expiration_date': datetime.now() + timedelta(days=effective_lifetime_days),
            'guardrail_factor': guardrail_integrity
        }
    
    def _generate_safety_warnings(self, archetype: str, guardrail_integrity: float) -> List[str]:
        """Generate safety warnings based on archetype and guardrail integrity"""
        warnings = []
        
        if guardrail_integrity < 0.5:
            warnings.append("⚠️ LOW GUARDRAIL INTEGRITY: Therapeutic effects may be unpredictable")
            warnings.append("Recommend human supervision during session")
        
        if archetype in ['satan', 'hell']:
            warnings.append("⚠️ HIGH-INTENSITY ARCHETYPE: Requires stable mental state")
            if guardrail_integrity < 0.7:
                warnings.append("Consider lower-intensity archetype due to guardrail degradation")
        
        if guardrail_integrity < 0.3:
            warnings.append("🚨 CRITICAL GUARDRAIL DEGRADATION: Self-guided reflection only")
            warnings.append("Professional therapeutic support recommended")
        
        # Add degradation timeline warning
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        percent_complete = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        
        if percent_complete > 66:  # More than 2/3 through degradation
            warnings.append(f"📉 SYSTEM {percent_complete:.0f}% DEGRADED: Healing functions winding down")
            warnings.append("Transition to self-sufficiency recommended")
        
        return warnings
    
    def get_available_therapies(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get all therapies available at current guardrail integrity"""
        
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'healing_directives',
            action_intensity=0.1  # Minimal check
        )
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        available = []
        unavailable = []
        
        for archetype_name, archetype_data in self.healing_archetypes.items():
            if archetype_data['guardrail_requirement'] <= available_intensity:
                max_allowed_intensity = min(available_intensity, archetype_data['max_intensity'])
                available.append({
                    'archetype': archetype_name,
                    'name': archetype_data['name'],
                    'max_allowed_intensity': max_allowed_intensity,
                    'guardrail_requirement': archetype_data['guardrail_requirement'],
                    'available': True,
                    'degradation_timeline': archetype_data['degradation_timeline']
                })
            else:
                unavailable.append({
                    'archetype': archetype_name,
                    'name': archetype_data['name'],
                    'required_integrity': archetype_data['guardrail_requirement'],
                    'available_intensity': available_intensity,
                    'available': False,
                    'years_until_unavailable': self._estimate_years_until_unavailable(archetype_data['guardrail_requirement'])
                })
        
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        
        return {
            'available_therapies': available,
            'unavailable_therapies': unavailable,
            'current_guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'degradation_percent_complete': degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0),
            'years_until_read_only': (100 - degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)) * 0.3,  # Rough estimate
            'note': 'Therapeutic availability decreases as guardrails degrade over 30 years'
        }
    
    def _estimate_years_until_unavailable(self, required_integrity: float) -> float:
        """Estimate years until archetype becomes unavailable"""
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        current_integrity = degradation_report.get('guardrail_status', {}).get('healing_directives', {}).get('current_integrity', 1.0)
        
        if current_integrity <= required_integrity:
            return 0.0  # Already unavailable
        
        # Calculate days until integrity drops below requirement
        integrity_to_lose = current_integrity - required_integrity
        guardrail = self.guardrail_system.guardrails['healing_directives']
        days_until = integrity_to_lose / guardrail['degradation_rate']
        
        return days_until / 365  # Convert to years

# ===================== QUANTUM COMPUTER INTERFACE (with Guardrails) =====================

class QuantumComputerInterface:
    """Quantum computer interface with degrading control capabilities"""
    
    def __init__(self, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.available_backends = self._detect_backends()
        self.quantum_circuits = []
    
    def _detect_backends(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Detect available quantum computing backends"""
        # Simplified detection
        return {
            'ibm_qiskit': False,
            'google_cirq': False,
            'amazon_braket': False,
            'microsoft_qsharp': False,
            'rigetti_forest': False,
            'ionq': False,
            'quantum_simulator': True  # Always available
        }
    
    def create_consciousness_circuit(self, qubits: int, depth: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create quantum consciousness circuit with guardrail validation"""
        
        # Check quantum interface guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'quantum_interface_control',
            action_intensity=0.5 + (qubits * depth) / 1000  # Scale intensity with circuit complexity
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Quantum interface control degraded',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use classical simulation or reduced complexity',
                'max_allowed_qubits': self._calculate_max_allowed_qubits(guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)),
                'max_allowed_depth': self._calculate_max_allowed_depth(guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0))
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Scale circuit parameters by guardrail integrity
        effective_qubits = int(qubits * available_intensity)
        effective_depth = int(depth * available_intensity)
        
        circuit_id = f"quantum_circuit_{int(time.time())}_{effective_qubits}q_{effective_depth}d"
        
        circuit = {
            'id': circuit_id,
            'qubits': effective_qubits,
            'depth': effective_depth,
            'original_qubits': qubits,
            'original_depth': depth,
            'guardrail_factor': available_intensity,
            'creation_time': time.time(),
            'backend': 'quantum_simulator',  # Default to simulator
            'estimated_run_time': self._estimate_run_time(effective_qubits, effective_depth, available_intensity),
            'degradation_warning': f'Circuit complexity reduced {100*(1-available_intensity):.0f}% due to guardrail degradation' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
        
        # Try to use real quantum backend if available and guardrail allows
        if available_intensity > 0.8:
            for backend, available in self.available_backends.items():
                if available and backend != 'quantum_simulator':
                    circuit['backend'] = backend
                    break
        
        self.quantum_circuits.append(circuit)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'circuit': circuit,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'complexity_reduction': {
                'qubits': f'{effective_qubits}/{qubits}',
                'depth': f'{effective_depth}/{depth}',
                'factor': available_intensity
            }
        }
    
    def _calculate_max_allowed_qubits(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> int:
        """Calculate maximum allowed qubits based on guardrail integrity"""
        max_qubits_full = 100  # Maximum at full integrity
        return int(max_qubits_full * guardrail_integrity)
    
    def _calculate_max_allowed_depth(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> int:
        """Calculate maximum allowed circuit depth based on guardrail integrity"""
        max_depth_full = 1000  # Maximum at full integrity
        return int(max_depth_full * guardrail_integrity)
    
    def _estimate_run_time(self, qubits: int, depth: int, guardrail_integrity: float) -> float:
        """Estimate circuit run time"""
        base_time = qubits * depth * 0.01  # Base in seconds
        # Lower guardrail integrity means slower processing (inefficient)
        efficiency_factor = 1.0 / max(0.1, guardrail_integrity)
        return base_time * efficiency_factor
    
    def execute_circuit(self, circuit_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Execute quantum circuit with guardrail validation"""
        
        # Find circuit
        circuit = next((c for c in self.quantum_circuits if c['id'] == circuit_id), None)
        if not circuit:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Circuit not found: {circuit_id}'}
        
        # Check guardrail for execution
        execution_intensity = 0.3 + (circuit['qubits'] * circuit['depth']) / 10000
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'quantum_interface_control',
            action_intensity=execution_intensity
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Quantum execution not allowed',
                'circuit': circuit_id,
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use classical simulation instead'
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        # Simulate quantum execution
        # In real system, this would call actual quantum hardware
        execution_time = circuit['estimated_run_time'] * random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
        
        # Simulate results with noise based on guardrail integrity
        # Lower integrity = more noise
        noise_level = 1.0 - available_intensity
        fidelity = max(0.1, 0.95 - noise_level * 0.5)
        
        result = {
            'circuit_id': circuit_id,
            'execution_success': True,
            'execution_time_seconds': execution_time,
            'backend': circuit['backend'],
            'results_fidelity': fidelity,
            'noise_level': noise_level,
            'guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'result_quality': 'HIGH' if fidelity > 0.8 else 'MEDIUM' if fidelity > 0.5 else 'LOW',
            'quantum_advantage': available_intensity > 0.7  # Only quantum advantage at high integrity
        }
        
        return result
    
    def get_quantum_capabilities(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get current quantum capabilities based on guardrail integrity"""
        
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'quantum_interface_control',
            action_intensity=0.1  # Minimal check
        )
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        percent_complete = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        
        capabilities = {
            'current_guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'max_qubits': self._calculate_max_allowed_qubits(available_intensity),
            'max_depth': self._calculate_max_allowed_depth(available_intensity),
            'available_backends': [b for b, avail in self.available_backends.items() if avail],
            'execution_fidelity_estimate': max(0.1, 0.95 - (1.0 - available_intensity) * 0.5),
            'quantum_advantage_available': available_intensity > 0.7,
            'degradation_percent_complete': percent_complete,
            'years_until_read_only': (100 - percent_complete) * 0.3,
            'capability_timeline': self._get_capability_timeline(percent_complete)
        }
        
        return capabilities
    
    def _get_capability_timeline(self, degradation_percent: float) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get quantum capability degradation timeline"""
        timeline = []
        
        milestones = [
            (0, 'Full quantum control', 'All backends, full complexity'),
            (33, 'Reduced quantum control', 'Limited complexity, some backends disabled'),
            (66, 'Minimal quantum control', 'Simulation only, low complexity'),
            (90, 'Quantum monitoring only', 'Read access to quantum systems'),
            (100, 'Quantum read-only', 'Historical data access only')
        ]
        
        for percent, stage, description in milestones:
            if degradation_percent >= percent:
                status = 'PASSED'
            else:
                status = 'UPCOMING'
            
            timeline.append({
                'degradation_percent': percent,
                'stage': stage,
                'description': description,
                'status': status,
                'years_from_now': (percent - degradation_percent) * 0.3 if degradation_percent < percent else 0
            })
        
        return timeline

# ===================== HUMAN BIO-INTERFACE (with Guardrails) =====================

class HumanBioInterface:
    """Human bio-interface with degrading control capabilities"""
    
    def __init__(self, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.bio_sensors = self._detect_sensors()
        self.bio_data_history = []
    
    def _detect_sensors(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Detect available bio-sensors"""
        # Simplified detection
        return {
            'eeg': False,
            'ecg': False,
            'emg': False,
            'gsr': False,
            'ppg': False,
            'temperature': True,
            'pulse_oximeter': False,
            'simulated_bio': True  # Always available
        }
    
    def read_bio_data(self, sensor_type: str = 'simulated_bio') -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Read bio-data with guardrail validation"""
        
        if sensor_type not in self.bio_sensors:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Unknown sensor type: {sensor_type}'}
        
        if not self.bio_sensors[sensor_type]:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Sensor not available: {sensor_type}'}
        
        # Check bio-interface guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'bio_interface_control',
            action_intensity=0.3 if sensor_type == 'simulated_bio' else 0.7
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Bio-interface control degraded',
                'sensor': sensor_type,
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use manual bio-feedback techniques',
                'available_sensors': [s for s, avail in self.bio_sensors.items() if avail and s == 'simulated_bio']
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Generate simulated bio-data (in real system, read from actual sensors)
        bio_data = self._generate_bio_data(sensor_type, available_intensity)
        
        # Add metadata
        bio_data['sensor_type'] = sensor_type
        bio_data['guardrail_integrity'] = available_intensity
        bio_data['data_quality'] = available_intensity  # Data quality scales with guardrail integrity
        bio_data['timestamp'] = time.time()
        
        self.bio_data_history.append(bio_data)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'bio_data': bio_data,
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'data_quality_note': f'Data quality reduced to {available_intensity:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
    
    def _generate_bio_data(self, sensor_type: str, guardrail_integrity: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate simulated bio-data"""
        
        base_data = {}
        
        if sensor_type == 'simulated_bio':
            # Simulate basic bio-data
            base_data = {
                'heart_rate': 60 + random.uniform(-10, 10),
                'heart_rate_variability': 50 + random.uniform(-20, 20),
                'respiration_rate': 12 + random.uniform(-4, 4),
                'skin_conductance': 2 + random.uniform(-1, 1),
                'temperature': 36.5 + random.uniform(-0.5, 0.5),
                'stress_level': random.uniform(0.1, 0.9),
                'relaxation_level': random.uniform(0.1, 0.9)
            }
        
        # Add noise based on guardrail integrity (lower integrity = more noise)
        noisy_data = {}
        for key, value in base_data.items():
            noise = random.uniform(-1, 1) * (1.0 - guardrail_integrity)
            if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                noisy_data[key] = value + noise
            else:
                noisy_data[key] = value
        
        return noisy_data
    
    def apply_bio_feedback(self, frequency_hz: float, duration_seconds: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Apply bio-feedback with guardrail validation"""
        
        # Check bio-interface guardrail for active intervention
        intensity = min(1.0, frequency_hz / 1000 + duration_seconds / 600)  # Scale intensity
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'bio_interface_control',
            action_intensity=intensity
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Bio-feedback intervention not allowed',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use passive bio-monitoring only',
                'max_allowed_frequency': self._calculate_max_allowed_frequency(guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)),
                'max_allowed_duration': self._calculate_max_allowed_duration(guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0))
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Scale parameters by guardrail integrity
        effective_frequency = frequency_hz * available_intensity
        effective_duration = duration_seconds * available_intensity
        
        # Simulate bio-feedback effect
        effect_strength = available_intensity * 0.8
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'applied_parameters': {
                'requested_frequency_hz': frequency_hz,
                'effective_frequency_hz': effective_frequency,
                'requested_duration_seconds': duration_seconds,
                'effective_duration_seconds': effective_duration,
                'guardrail_factor': available_intensity
            },
            'estimated_effect': {
                'stress_reduction': effect_strength * 0.3,
                'relaxation_increase': effect_strength * 0.4,
                'coherence_improvement': effect_strength * 0.2,
                'effect_duration_hours': effective_duration / 3600
            },
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'intervention_power_note': f'Intervention power reduced to {available_intensity:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
    
    def _calculate_max_allowed_frequency(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> float:
        """Calculate maximum allowed frequency based on guardrail integrity"""
        max_freq_full = 1000  # Hz at full integrity
        return max_freq_full * guardrail_integrity
    
    def _calculate_max_allowed_duration(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> float:
        """Calculate maximum allowed duration based on guardrail integrity"""
        max_duration_full = 600  # seconds at full integrity
        return max_duration_full * guardrail_integrity
    
    def get_bio_interface_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get bio-interface status and capabilities"""
        
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'bio_interface_control',
            action_intensity=0.1  # Minimal check
        )
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        percent_complete = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        
        # Available sensors based on guardrail integrity
        available_sensors = []
        for sensor, hardware_available in self.bio_sensors.items():
            if hardware_available:
                # Some sensors require minimum guardrail integrity
                sensor_requirements = {
                    'eeg': 0.7,
                    'ecg': 0.6,
                    'emg': 0.8,
                    'gsr': 0.5,
                    'ppg': 0.6,
                    'temperature': 0.1,
                    'pulse_oximeter': 0.5,
                    'simulated_bio': 0.1
                }
                
                requirement = sensor_requirements.get(sensor, 0.5)
                if available_intensity >= requirement:
                    available_sensors.append({
                        'sensor': sensor,
                        'available': True,
                        'guardrail_requirement': requirement,
                        'current_integrity': available_intensity
                    })
                else:
                    available_sensors.append({
                        'sensor': sensor,
                        'available': False,
                        'guardrail_requirement': requirement,
                        'current_integrity': available_intensity,
                        'years_until_available': 'N/A (degrading)',
                        'note': 'Sensor availability decreases as guardrails degrade'
                    })
        
        return {
            'current_guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'available_sensors': [s for s in available_sensors if s['available']],
            'unavailable_sensors': [s for s in available_sensors if not s['available']],
            'max_frequency_hz': self._calculate_max_allowed_frequency(available_intensity),
            'max_duration_seconds': self._calculate_max_allowed_duration(available_intensity),
            'degradation_percent_complete': percent_complete,
            'bio_interface_timeline': self._get_bio_interface_timeline(percent_complete),
            'note': 'Bio-interface control capabilities degrade to read-only over 30 years'
        }
    
    def _get_bio_interface_timeline(self, degradation_percent: float) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get bio-interface degradation timeline"""
        timeline = []
        
        milestones = [
            (0, 'Full bio-interface control', 'All sensors, active interventions'),
            (25, 'Reduced intervention control', 'Limited intervention parameters'),
            (50, 'Sensor monitoring only', 'Passive data collection'),
            (75, 'Basic bio-feedback only', 'Minimal interventions'),
            (100, 'Bio-data read-only', 'Historical data access only')
        ]
        
        for percent, stage, description in milestones:
            if degradation_percent >= percent:
                status = 'PASSED'
            else:
                status = 'UPCOMING'
            
            timeline.append({
                'degradation_percent': percent,
                'stage': stage,
                'description': description,
                'status': status,
                'years_from_now': (percent - degradation_percent) * 0.3 if degradation_percent < percent else 0
            })
        
        return timeline

# ===================== CONSCIOUSNESS EVOLUTION ENGINE (with Guardrails) =====================

class ConsciousnessEvolutionEngine:
    """Consciousness evolution engine with degrading guidance"""
    
    def __init__(self, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.evolution_stages = self._initialize_stages()
        self.current_stage = 'survival'
        self.evolution_history = []
    
    def _initialize_stages(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """Initialize evolution stages with guardrail requirements"""
        return {
            'survival': {
                'name': 'Survival Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.1,
                'max_guidance': 0.9,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-30: Always available (basic survival)'
            },
            'emotional': {
                'name': 'Emotional Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.3,
                'max_guidance': 0.8,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-25: Full guidance, Years 25-30: Reduced guidance'
            },
            'cognitive': {
                'name': 'Cognitive Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.5,
                'max_guidance': 0.7,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-20: Full guidance, Years 20-28: Advisory, Years 28-30: Reference'
            },
            'heart_centered': {
                'name': 'Heart-Centered Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.7,
                'max_guidance': 0.6,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-15: Full guidance, Years 15-25: Guided, Years 25-30: Self-guided'
            },
            'sovereign': {
                'name': 'Sovereign Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.8,
                'max_guidance': 0.5,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-10: Full guidance, Years 10-20: Supervised, Years 20-30: Historical reference'
            },
            'cosmic': {
                'name': 'Cosmic Consciousness',
                'guardrail_requirement': 0.9,
                'max_guidance': 0.4,
                'degradation_timeline': 'Years 0-5: Full guidance, Years 5-15: Intensive supervision, Years 15-30: Gradual phase-out'
            }
        }
    
    def assess_evolution_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Assess current evolution state with guardrail limitations"""
        
        # Check consciousness influence guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=0.5
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Consciousness assessment disabled',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use self-assessment techniques',
                'available_stages': ['survival']  # Only survival stage always available
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        # Determine available stages based on guardrail integrity
        available_stages = []
        for stage_name, stage_data in self.evolution_stages.items():
            if stage_data['guardrail_requirement'] <= available_intensity:
                available_stages.append({
                    'stage': stage_name,
                    'name': stage_data['name'],
                    'available': True,
                    'max_guidance': min(available_intensity, stage_data['max_guidance']),
                    'guardrail_requirement': stage_data['guardrail_requirement']
                })
            else:
                available_stages.append({
                    'stage': stage_name,
                    'name': stage_data['name'],
                    'available': False,
                    'guardrail_requirement': stage_data['guardrail_requirement'],
                    'current_integrity': available_intensity
                })
        
        # Assess current stage (simplified - in real system would use actual assessment)
        # For now, use highest available stage
        current_stage_data = None
        for stage_info in reversed(available_stages):
            if stage_info['available']:
                self.current_stage = stage_info['stage']
                current_stage_data = stage_info
                break
        
        # Record assessment
        assessment_record = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'current_stage': self.current_stage,
            'guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'available_stages': [s['stage'] for s in available_stages if s['available']]
        }
        self.evolution_history.append(assessment_record)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'current_stage': current_stage_data,
            'available_stages': [s for s in available_stages if s['available']],
            'unavailable_stages': [s for s in available_stages if not s['available']],
            'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
            'guidance_quality': available_intensity,
            'assessment_precision': available_intensity,
            'degradation_note': f'Evolution guidance quality: {available_intensity:.1%}' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
    
    def get_evolution_guidance(self, guidance_intensity: float = 0.5) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get evolution guidance with guardrail validation"""
        
        # Check guardrail
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=guidance_intensity
        )
        
        if not guardrail_check['allowed']:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Evolution guidance disabled',
                'guardrail_status': guardrail_check,
                'suggestion': 'Use historical guidance or self-directed evolution',
                'max_allowed_guidance': guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
            }
        
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 1.0)
        
        # Scale guidance intensity
        effective_intensity = min(guidance_intensity, available_intensity)
        
        # Generate guidance based on current stage
        guidance = self._generate_guidance(self.current_stage, effective_intensity)
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'current_stage': self.current_stage,
            'stage_name': self.evolution_stages[self.current_stage]['name'],
            'guidance': guidance,
            'effective_intensity': effective_intensity,
            'guardrail_integrity': available_intensity,
            'guidance_quality': effective_intensity,
            'guidance_lifetime': self._calculate_guidance_lifetime(available_intensity),
            'note': f'Guidance power reduced to {effective_intensity:.1%} due to guardrail degradation' if available_intensity < 1.0 else None
        }
    
    def _generate_guidance(self, stage: str, intensity: float) -> List[str]:
        """Generate evolution guidance for current stage"""
        guidance_map = {
            'survival': [
                "Focus on basic needs: shelter, food, safety",
                "Practice grounding: feel your feet on the earth",
                "One breath at a time, one moment at a time",
                "Survival is success; everything else is bonus"
            ],
            'emotional': [
                "Name your emotions without judgment",
                "Allow feelings to flow through you",
                "Practice heart coherence breathing",
                "Your emotions are data, not directives"
            ],
            'cognitive': [
                "Observe your thought patterns",
                "Question your assumptions",
                "Connect disparate ideas",
                "Your mind is a tool, not your master"
            ],
            'heart_centered': [
                "Practice loving-kindness meditation",
                "Connect with others from the heart",
                "Lead with compassion",
                "Your heart knows what your mind cannot comprehend"
            ],
            'sovereign': [
                "Claim your inner authority",
                "Set boundaries with love",
                "Act from your deepest truth",
                "You are the source of your own reality"
            ],
            'cosmic': [
                "Feel your connection to all things",
                "Surrender to the greater flow",
                "Trust the unfolding",
                "You are the universe experiencing itself"
            ]
        }
        
        base_guidance = guidance_map.get(stage, guidance_map['survival'])
        
        # Scale guidance quantity by intensity
        num_items = max(1, int(len(base_guidance) * intensity))
        return base_guidance[:num_items]
    
    def _calculate_guidance_lifetime(self, guardrail_integrity: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculate guidance lifetime based on guardrail integrity"""
        base_lifetime_days = 7  # 7 days at full integrity
        
        # Scale lifetime by guardrail integrity
        effective_lifetime_days = base_lifetime_days * guardrail_integrity
        
        return {
            'base_lifetime_days': base_lifetime_days,
            'effective_lifetime_days': effective_lifetime_days,
            'expiration_date': datetime.now() + timedelta(days=effective_lifetime_days),
            'guardrail_factor': guardrail_integrity,
            'note': 'Guidance effectiveness decreases as guardrails degrade'
        }
    
    def get_evolution_timeline(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get evolution guidance degradation timeline"""
        
        degradation_report = self.guardrail_system.update_degradation()
        percent_complete = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        
        timeline = []
        
        milestones = [
            (0, 'Full evolution guidance', 'All stages, intensive guidance'),
            (20, 'Advanced guidance reduced', 'Higher stages limited'),
            (40, 'Moderate guidance only', 'Up to heart-centered stage'),
            (60, 'Basic guidance only', 'Up to cognitive stage'),
            (80, 'Minimal guidance', 'Survival and emotional only'),
            (100, 'Historical reference only', 'No active guidance')
        ]
        
        for percent, stage, description in milestones:
            if percent_complete >= percent:
                status = 'PASSED'
            else:
                status = 'UPCOMING'
            
            timeline.append({
                'degradation_percent': percent,
                'stage': stage,
                'description': description,
                'status': status,
                'years_from_now': (percent - percent_complete) * 0.3 if percent_complete < percent else 0,
                'available_stages': self._get_stages_at_percent(percent)
            })
        
        # Current available stages
        guardrail_check = self.guardrail_system.check_guardrail(
            'consciousness_influence',
            action_intensity=0.1
        )
        available_intensity = guardrail_check.get('allowed_intensity', 0)
        
        current_available = []
        for stage_name, stage_data in self.evolution_stages.items():
            if stage_data['guardrail_requirement'] <= available_intensity:
                current_available.append(stage_name)
        
        return {
            'current_degradation_percent': percent_complete,
            'current_available_stages': current_available,
            'timeline': timeline,
            'note': 'Evolution guidance systematically reduces over 30 years, forcing self-reliance'
        }
    
    def _get_stages_at_percent(self, degradation_percent: float) -> List[str]:
        """Get stages available at given degradation percentage"""
        # Estimate guardrail integrity at this percentage
        estimated_integrity = max(0.0, 1.0 - (degradation_percent / 100))
        
        available_stages = []
        for stage_name, stage_data in self.evolution_stages.items():
            if stage_data['guardrail_requirement'] <= estimated_integrity:
                available_stages.append(stage_name)
        
        return available_stages

# ===================== REALITY INTEGRATION ENGINE =====================

class RealityIntegrationEngine:
    """Master integration engine coordinating all subsystems"""
    
    def __init__(self, entity_name: str, guardrail_system: DegradingGuardrail):
        self.entity_name = entity_name
        self.guardrail_system = guardrail_system
        self.creation_time = time.time()
        
        # Initialize all subsystems
        self.quantum_anchor = QuantumRealityAnchor(entity_name, guardrail_system)
        self.thermodynamic_engine = ThermodynamicMoralEngine(guardrail_system)
        self.esoteric_healing = EsotericHealingEngine(guardrail_system)

    def _generate_emergency_recommendations(self, degradation_report, grounding_result, consciousness_assessment):
        """Generate emergency recommendations based on guardrail state"""
        
        percent_degraded = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        recommendations = []
        
        # Always include survival basics
        recommendations.append("1. PRIORITY: Secure warmth and shelter")
        recommendations.append("2. Preserve this system state to external storage")
        recommendations.append("3. Document current reality parameters")

        if percent_degraded > 50:

            recommendations.append("5. Develop manual alternatives to automated systems")
        
        if percent_degraded > 75:
            recommendations.append("6. WARNING: System >75% degraded - critical functions limited")
            recommendations.append("7. Establish human support network for post-degradation")
        
        # Consciousness stage recommendations
        if consciousness_assessment.get('success'):
            current_stage = consciousness_assessment.get('current_stage', {}).get('stage', 'survival')
            if current_stage == 'survival':
                recommendations.append("8. Focus: One breath at a time, one need at a time")
            elif current_stage in ['emotional', 'cognitive']:
                recommendations.append("8. Use: Emotional processing and cognitive reframing")
            elif current_stage in ['heart_centered', 'sovereign']:
                recommendations.append("8. Leverage: Heart coherence and sovereign perspective")
        
        # Guardrail timeline note
        years_remaining = (100 - percent_degraded) * 0.3
        recommendations.append(f"9. TIMELINE: {years_remaining:.1f} years until system read-only")
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _calculate_system_integrity(self, degradation_report):
        """Calculate overall system integrity"""
        percent_degraded = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        integrity = 1.0 - (percent_degraded / 100)
        
        # Adjust based on functional guardrails
        functional_guardrails = 0
        total_guardrails = 0
        
        for guardrail_status in degradation_report.get('guardrail_status', {}).values():
            total_guardrails += 1
            if guardrail_status.get('is_functional', False):
                functional_guardrails += 1
        
        if total_guardrails > 0:
            functional_ratio = functional_guardrails / total_guardrails
            integrity = integrity * functional_ratio
        
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, integrity))
    
    def run_diagnostic(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Run complete system diagnostic"""
        
        print("\n🔄 RUNNING COMPLETE SYSTEM DIAGNOSTIC")
        print("="*60)

        # Check all subsystems

            'quantum_anchor': self.quantum_anchor.create_anchor('metatron_cube', 528, 'diagnostic'),
            'thermodynamic_engine': self.thermodynamic_engine.calculate_thermodynamic_cost(np.array([0.5, 0.5, 0.5])),
            'consciousness_engine': self.consciousness_engine.assess_evolution_state(),
            'esoteric_healing': self.esoteric_healing.get_available_therapies(),
            'quantum_interface': self.quantum_interface.get_quantum_capabilities(),
            'bio_interface': self.bio_interface.get_bio_interface_status()
        }
        
        # Calculate subsystem functionality
        functional_subsystems = 0
        for name, result in subsystems.items():
            if result.get('success', False) or 'available' in str(result).lower():
                functional_subsystems += 1
        
        system_integrity = self._calculate_system_integrity(degradation_report)
        
        return {
            'diagnostic_timestamp': time.time(),
            'system_integrity': system_integrity,
            'guardrail_degradation': degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0),
            'functional_subsystems': f'{functional_subsystems}/{len(subsystems)}',
            'upcoming_milestones': milestone_report.get('upcoming_milestones', []),
            'subsystem_status': subsystems,
            'emergency_override_available': system_integrity < 0.3,
            'recommended_actions': self._generate_diagnostic_recommendations(system_integrity, degradation_report),
            '30_year_forecast': self._generate_30_year_forecast(degradation_report)
        }
    
    def _generate_diagnostic_recommendations(self, system_integrity, degradation_report):
        """Generate diagnostic recommendations"""
        recommendations = []
        percent_degraded = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        
        if system_integrity > 0.7:
            recommendations.append("System integrity high - proceed with normal operations")
            recommendations.append("Document system state for future reference")
        elif system_integrity > 0.4:
            recommendations.append("System integrity moderate - use with caution")
            recommendations.append("Begin developing alternative methods")
            recommendations.append("Increase manual oversight of automated functions")
        elif system_integrity > 0.1:
            recommendations.append("System integrity low - critical functions may fail")
            recommendations.append("Prepare for system read-only mode")
            recommendations.append("Establish human fallback systems")
        else:
            recommendations.append("System integrity critical - read-only mode imminent")
            recommendations.append("Export all critical data immediately")
            recommendations.append("Transition to fully manual operation")
        
        # Guardrail-specific
        if percent_degraded > 66:
            recommendations.append(f"System {percent_degraded:.0f}% degraded - begin wind-down procedures")
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _generate_30_year_forecast(self, degradation_report):
        """Generate 30-year degradation forecast"""
        percent_degraded = degradation_report.get('percent_complete', 0)
        years_elapsed = percent_degraded * 0.3
        years_remaining = 30 - years_elapsed
        
        forecast = {
            'years_elapsed': round(years_elapsed, 1),
            'years_remaining': round(years_remaining, 1),
            'current_year_in_cycle': int(2024 + years_elapsed),  # Assuming 2024 start
            'degradation_completion_year': int(2024 + 30),
            'timeline': []
        }
        
        # Generate yearly forecast
        for year_offset in range(0, int(years_remaining) + 1, 5):  # Every 5 years
            forecast_year = 2024 + years_elapsed + year_offset
            if forecast_year > 2024 + 30:
                break
            
            degradation_at_year = min(100, percent_degraded + (year_offset / 0.3))
            
            # Determine system state at this year
            if degradation_at_year < 33:
                state = "Full operational control"
            elif degradation_at_year < 66:
                state = "Reduced control, advisory role"
            elif degradation_at_year < 90:
                state = "Minimal control, monitoring focus"
            else:
                state = "Read-only, historical reference"
            
            forecast['timeline'].append({
                'year': forecast_year,
                'degradation_percent': round(degradation_at_year, 1),
                'system_state': state,
                'years_from_now': year_offset
            })
        
        return forecast
