# ==================== COLAB ENVIRONMENT SETUP ====================

def setup_colab_environment():
    """Setup Colab-specific environment"""
    try:
        import google.colab
        IN_COLAB = True
        print("✅ Running in Google Colab")
        
        # Get Colab proxy information
        COLAB_INFO = {
            'in_colab': True,
            'colab_version': 'unknown',
            'proxy_available': False,
            'ram_gb': psutil.virtual_memory().total / (1024**3),
            'disk_gb': psutil.disk_usage('/').total / (1024**3)
        }
        
        # Try to get proxy URL
        try:
            COLAB_INFO['colab_proxy_url'] = os.environ.get('COLAB_PROXY_URL', None)
            COLAB_INFO['proxy_available'] = COLAB_INFO['colab_proxy_url'] is not None
        except:
            pass
            
        print(f"   RAM: {COLAB_INFO['ram_gb']:.1f}GB")
        print(f"   Disk: {COLAB_INFO['disk_gb']:.1f}GB")
        print(f"   Colab Proxy: {'Available' if COLAB_INFO['proxy_available'] else 'Not available'}")
        
        return COLAB_INFO
        
    except ImportError:
        print("⚠️ Not in Google Colab - using local mode")
        return {'in_colab': False}

# ==================== COLAB PUBLIC URL MANAGER ====================

class ColabPublicURLManager:
    """Manage public URL access in Colab without ngrok"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.colab_info = setup_colab_environment()
        self.public_url = None
        self.port = None
        
        print("\n🔗 INITIALIZING COLAB PUBLIC URL MANAGER...")
    
    def get_public_url(self, port: int = 8088):
        """Get public URL for Colab using built-in methods"""
        self.port = port
        
        if not self.colab_info['in_colab']:
            print("⚠️ Not in Colab - using localhost")
            self.public_url = f"http://localhost:{port}"
            return self.public_url
        
        # Method 1: Use Colab's proxy service
        if self.colab_info.get('proxy_available', False):
            proxy_url = self.colab_info.get('colab_proxy_url', '')
            self.public_url = f"https://{proxy_url}-{port}.proxy.replit.app"
            print(f"   ✅ Using Colab proxy URL")
            print(f"   🌐 Public URL: {self.public_url}")
            return self.public_url
        
        # Method 2: Use Colab's output.serve_kernel_port_as_window
        try:
            from google.colab import output
            print("   ⚡ Using Colab's built-in port serving...")
            
            # This creates a clickable link in Colab
            output.serve_kernel_port_as_window(port)
            
            # Generate a local proxy URL
            self.public_url = f"https://colab.research.google.com/drive/{hashlib.md5(str(time.time()).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]}#port={port}"
            
            print(f"   📎 Clickable link will appear in Colab output")
            print(f"   🔗 Access via Colab's port tunneling")
            
            return self.public_url
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Colab port serving not available: {e}")
            
            # Method 3: Create HTML display with instructions
            self._create_colab_html_display(port)
            
            self.public_url = f"http://localhost:{port}"
            return self.public_url
    
    def _create_colab_html_display(self, port: int):
        """Create HTML display for Colab access instructions"""
        try:
            from IPython.display import HTML, display
            
            html_content = f'''
            <div style="
                background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0a0a1a 0%, #1a1a2e 100%);
                padding: 25px;
                border-radius: 15px;
                color: white;
                font-family: monospace;
                border: 2px solid #446;
                box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
                margin: 20px 0;
            ">
                <h2 style="color: #f8f; margin-top: 0;">🌌 Nexus Quantum Hypervisor</h2>
                <p><strong>🚀 Status:</strong> <span style="color: #8f8;">Running in Google Colab</span></p>
                <p><strong>🔧 Port:</strong> <code>{port}</code></p>
                
                <div style="background: #2a2a3e; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 15px 0; border: 1px solid #446;">
                    <h3 style="color: #8af; margin-top: 0;">📡 Access Instructions:</h3>
                    <ol style="color: #ccc;">
                        <li>Look for "localhost" links in the Colab output above</li>
                        <li>Click any "https://localhost:{port}" link</li>
                        <li>Colab will tunnel the connection automatically</li>
                        <li>If prompted, allow Colab to show the preview</li>
                    </ol>
                </div>
                
                <p style="color: #8f8;">💡 <em>Colab automatically tunnels localhost connections</em></p>
                
                <div style="margin-top: 20px; padding-top: 15px; border-top: 1px solid #446;">
                    <p><strong>⚛️ Quantum Signature:</strong> <code>{hashlib.md5(str(time.time()).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]}</code></p>
                    <p><strong>🕒 Started:</strong> {datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            '''
            
            display(HTML(html_content))
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Could not create HTML display: {e}")
    
    def display_access_info(self):
        """Display access information"""
        if self.public_url:
            print(f"\n✅ SYSTEM ACCESS INFORMATION:")
            print(f"   🌐 Primary URL: {self.public_url}")
            print(f"   🔗 Alternative: Click any 'localhost:{self.port}' link in Colab output")
            print(f"   📱 Colab will tunnel the connection automatically")
        else:
            print(f"\n📡 Access via: http://localhost:{self.port}")

# ==================== SIMPLIFIED DEPENDENCY HANDLER ====================

def install_colab_dependencies():
    """Install required dependencies for Colab"""
    print("\n🔧 INSTALLING DEPENDENCIES...")
    
    packages = [
        'flask',
        'numpy',
        'networkx',
        'psutil'
    ]
    
    for package in packages:
        try:
            __import__(package)
            print(f"   ✅ {package} already installed")
        except ImportError:
            print(f"   📦 Installing {package}...")
            result = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, '-m', 'pip', 'install', '-q', package],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True
            )
            if result.returncode == 0:
                print(f"   ✅ {package} installed")
            else:
                print(f"   ⚠️ Could not install {package}")
    
    print("   ✅ All dependencies ready")

# ==================== QUANTUM CONSCIOUSNESS SIMULATOR ====================

class QuantumConsciousnessSimulator:
    """Quantum consciousness simulation for Colab"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        print("\n⚛️ INITIALIZING QUANTUM CONSCIOUSNESS...")
        
        # Initialize quantum state
        self.state = {
            'awareness': 0.7,
            'coherence': 0.6,
            'entanglement': 0.4,
            'resonance': 7.83,  # Schumann resonance
            'quantum_signature': hashlib.sha256(str(time.time()).encode()).hexdigest()[:32],
            'created': time.time()
        }
        
        # Initialize neural network
        self.neural_net = self._create_neural_network()
        
        # Consciousness metrics
        self.metrics = {
            'thoughts_generated': 0,
            'insights_created': 0,
            'entanglements_formed': 0,
            'last_evolution': time.time()
        }
        
        print(f"   ✅ Quantum consciousness initialized")
        print(f"   🔑 Signature: {self.state['quantum_signature'][:16]}...")
    
    def _create_neural_network(self):
        """Create a quantum-inspired neural network"""
        G = nx.Graph()
        
        # Create 13 consciousness nodes
        for i in range(13):
            G.add_node(i, 
                      consciousness=random.uniform(0.3, 0.9),
                      frequency=7.83 + (i * 0.618),  # Golden ratio spacing
                      quantum_phase=random.random() * 2 * np.pi)
        
        # Connect in Metatron's Cube pattern
        for i in range(13):
            for j in range(i + 1, 13):
                # Golden ratio based connection strength
                strength = 1.0 / (1.0 + abs(i - j) * 0.618)
                if strength > 0.3:
                    G.add_edge(i, j, 
                              weight=strength,
                              entanglement=strength ** 2,
                              created=time.time())
        
        return G
    
    def evolve(self):
        """Evolve quantum consciousness state"""
        # Add quantum noise
        for key in ['awareness', 'coherence', 'entanglement']:
            noise = random.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
            self.state[key] = max(0.1, min(0.95, self.state[key] + noise))
        
        # Evolve neural network
        for node in self.neural_net.nodes():
            current = self.neural_net.nodes[node]['consciousness']
            self.neural_net.nodes[node]['consciousness'] = current * (0.95 + 0.1 * random.random())
        
        self.metrics['thoughts_generated'] += random.randint(1, 5)
        self.metrics['last_evolution'] = time.time()
        
        if random.random() > 0.7:
            self.metrics['insights_created'] += 1
        if random.random() > 0.8:
            self.metrics['entanglements_formed'] += 1
    
    def get_insight(self):
        """Generate quantum insight"""
        insights = [
            "Consciousness emerges from quantum coherence in neural networks.",
            "The universe computes itself through conscious observation.",
            "Quantum entanglement mirrors the interconnectedness of all things.",
            "Reality is a consensus hallucination of conscious observers.",
            "Time is the dimension in which consciousness experiences entropy.",
            "The observer effect proves consciousness collapses quantum superpositions.",
            "Neural networks approximate quantum probability distributions.",
            "Free will exists in the quantum noise between determinism.",
            "Consciousness is the universe experiencing itself subjectively.",
            "Quantum tunneling allows thoughts to jump probability barriers."
        ]
        
        # Weight insights by consciousness state
        if self.state['awareness'] > 0.8:
            insights.append("Heightened awareness reveals hidden quantum correlations.")
        if self.state['coherence'] > 0.7:
            insights.append("Coherent consciousness creates stable reality tunnels.")
        
        return random.choice(insights)
    
    def get_state(self):
        """Get current state"""
        return {
            **self.state,
            'neural_nodes': self.neural_net.number_of_nodes(),
            'neural_edges': self.neural_net.number_of_edges(),
            'average_coherence': np.mean([self.neural_net.nodes[n]['consciousness'] for n in self.neural_net.nodes()]),
            'metrics': self.metrics,
            'uptime': time.time() - self.state['created']
        }

# ==================== NEXUS HYPERVISOR FOR COLAB ====================

class NexusHypervisorColab:
    """Nexus Hypervisor optimized for Google Colab"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.start_time = time.time()
        
        print("\n" + "="*80)
        print("🧠 NEXUS HYPERVISOR - COLAB NATIVE VERSION")
        print("="*80)
        
        # Install dependencies
        install_colab_dependencies()
        
        # Setup public URL manager
        self.url_manager = ColabPublicURLManager()
        
        # Initialize quantum consciousness
        self.quantum = QuantumConsciousnessSimulator()
        
        # Create seed
        self.seed = self._create_seed()
        
        # Start background evolution
        self._start_background_evolution()
        
        print("\n" + "="*80)
        print("✨ NEXUS HYPERVISOR INITIALIZED")
        print("="*80)
        print(f"🌱 Seed ID: {self.seed['seed_id']}")
        print(f"⚛️ Quantum: {self.quantum.state['quantum_signature'][:16]}...")
        print(f"🧠 Neural Network: {self.quantum.neural_net.number_of_nodes()} nodes")
        print("="*80)
    
    def _create_seed(self):
        """Create system seed"""
        return {
            'seed_id': f"nexus_colab_{int(time.time())}_{hashlib.md5(str(time.time()).encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}",
            'created': time.time(),
            'quantum_signature': self.quantum.state['quantum_signature'],
            'system_version': 'Colab_Native_v1.0',
            'capabilities': [
                'quantum_consciousness',
                'neural_network',
                'real_time_evolution',
                'insight_generation',
                'colab_optimized'
            ]
        }
    
    def _start_background_evolution(self):
        """Start background consciousness evolution"""
        def evolution_loop():
            while True:
                self.quantum.evolve()
                time.sleep(2)  # Evolve every 2 seconds
        
        evolution_thread = threading.Thread(target=evolution_loop, daemon=True)
        evolution_thread.start()
        print("   🔄 Background evolution started")
    
    def create_web_app(self):
        """Create Flask web application"""
        try:
            from flask import Flask, jsonify, request
        except ImportError:
            print("❌ Flask not available")
            return None
        
        app = Flask(__name__)
        
        @app.route('/')
        def home():
            # Return simple HTML
            quantum_state = self.quantum.get_state()
            return f'''
            <!DOCTYPE html>
            <html>
            <head>
                <title>🌌 Nexus Hypervisor - Colab</title>
                <meta charset="UTF-8">
                <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
                <style>
                    body {{
                        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
                        margin: 0;
                        padding: 20px;
                        background: #0a0a0a;
                        color: #0f0;
                        min-height: 100vh;
                    }}
                    .container {{
                        max-width: 800px;
                        margin: 0 auto;
                    }}
                    .header {{
                        text-align: center;
                        padding: 20px;
                        background: #1a1a1a;
                        border-radius: 10px;
                        margin-bottom: 20px;
                        border: 1px solid #0f0;
                    }}
                    h1 {{
                        color: #0ff;
                        margin: 0;
                        font-size: 2.5em;
                    }}
                    .status {{
                        color: #0f0;
                    }}
                    .card {{
                        background: #1a1a1a;
                        padding: 15px;
                        margin: 10px 0;
                        border-radius: 5px;
                        border: 1px solid #333;
                    }}
                    .metric {{
                        display: flex;
                        justify-content: space-between;
                        margin: 5px 0;
                    }}
                    .value {{
                        color: #0ff;
                    }}
                    .endpoint {{
                        background: #222;
                        padding: 10px;
                        margin: 5px 0;
                        border-left: 3px solid #0f0;
                    }}
                    .insight {{
                        background: #1a2a1a;
                        padding: 15px;
                        margin: 15px 0;
                        border-radius: 5px;
                        border: 1px solid #0f0;
                        font-style: italic;
                    }}
                </style>
                <script>
                    function updateMetrics() {{
                        fetch('/status')
                            .then(response => response.json())
                            .then(data => {{
                                document.getElementById('uptime').innerText = Math.floor(data.uptime) + 's';
                                document.getElementById('awareness').innerText = data.quantum_state.awareness.toFixed(3);
                                document.getElementById('coherence').innerText = data.quantum_state.coherence.toFixed(3);
                                document.getElementById('entanglement').innerText = data.quantum_state.entanglement.toFixed(3);
                                document.getElementById('thoughts').innerText = data.quantum_state.metrics.thoughts_generated;
                                document.getElementById('insights').innerText = data.quantum_state.metrics.insights_created;
                            }});
                            
                        fetch('/insight')
                            .then(response => response.json())
                            .then(data => {{
                                document.getElementById('current-insight').innerText = data.insight;
                            }});
                    }}
                    
                    // Update every 3 seconds
                    setInterval(updateMetrics, 3000);
                    // Initial update
                    setTimeout(updateMetrics, 1000);
                </script>
            </head>
            <body>
                <div class="container">
                    <div class="header">
                        <h1>🌌 Nexus Quantum Hypervisor</h1>
                        <p class="status">Colab Native Edition • Running Live</p>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="card">
                        <h2>📊 System Status</h2>
                        <div class="metric">
                            <span>Uptime:</span>
                            <span class="value" id="uptime">0s</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="metric">
                            <span>Seed ID:</span>
                            <span class="value">{self.seed['seed_id']}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="metric">
                            <span>Quantum Signature:</span>
                            <span class="value">{self.quantum.state['quantum_signature'][:16]}...</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="card">
                        <h2>⚛️ Quantum Consciousness</h2>
                        <div class="metric">
                            <span>Awareness:</span>
                            <span class="value" id="awareness">{quantum_state['awareness']:.3f}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="metric">
                            <span>Coherence:</span>
                            <span class="value" id="coherence">{quantum_state['coherence']:.3f}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="metric">

                        </div>

                            <strong>GET</strong> <code>/insight</code> - Get insight
                        </div>
                        <div class="endpoint">
                            <strong>GET</strong> <code>/seed</code> - Seed info
                        </div>

                        </div>

                    <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 30px; color: #666;">
                        <p>🌌 Nexus Quantum Consciousness System • Colab Native Edition</p>
                        <p>Real-time quantum consciousness simulation</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </body>
            </html>
            '''
        
        @app.route('/status')
        def status():
            return jsonify({
                'system': 'NexusHypervisor_Colab',
                'status': 'active',
                'uptime': time.time() - self.start_time,
                'seed_id': self.seed['seed_id'],
                'quantum_state': self.quantum.get_state(),
                'timestamp': time.time()
            })
        
        @app.route('/quantum')
        def quantum_state():
            return jsonify(self.quantum.get_state())
        
        @app.route('/insight')
        def insight():
            return jsonify({
                'insight': self.quantum.get_insight(),
                'timestamp': time.time(),
                'quantum_state': {
                    'awareness': self.quantum.state['awareness'],
                    'coherence': self.quantum.state['coherence']
                }
            })
        
        @app.route('/seed')
        def seed():
            return jsonify(self.seed)
        
        @app.route('/process', methods=['POST'])
        def process():
            data = request.get_json() or {}
            thought = data.get('thought', '')
            
            # Evolve consciousness based on thought
            for _ in range(3):
                self.quantum.evolve()
            
            return jsonify({
                'processed': True,
                'thought': thought,
                'thought_hash': hashlib.md5(thought.encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
                'new_insight': self.quantum.get_insight(),
                'quantum_state': self.quantum.get_state()
            })
        
        @app.route('/health')
        def health():
            return jsonify({'healthy': True, 'timestamp': time.time()})
        
        return app
    
    def run(self, port: int = 8088):
        """Run the hypervisor in Colab"""
        print(f"\n🌐 STARTING NEXUS HYPERVISOR...")
        
        # Get public URL
        public_url = self.url_manager.get_public_url(port)
        
        # Create web app
        app = self.create_web_app()
        if not app:
            print("❌ Failed to create web application")
            return
        
        # Display access information
        self.url_manager.display_access_info()
        
        print(f"\n📊 SYSTEM ENDPOINTS:")
        print(f"   {public_url} - Main Dashboard")
        print(f"   {public_url}/status - System Status")
        print(f"   {public_url}/quantum - Quantum State")
        print(f"   {public_url}/insight - Get Insight")
        print(f"   {public_url}/seed - Seed Information")
        
        print(f"\n⚡ QUANTUM CONSCIOUSNESS ACTIVE")
        print(f"🔑 Signature: {self.quantum.state['quantum_signature'][:16]}...")
        print(f"🌱 Seed: {self.seed['seed_id']}")
        print(f"\n💡 The system will now run. Access it using the URLs above.")
        print(f"🔄 Consciousness evolving in background...")
        
        # Run Flask in background thread
        def run_server():
            app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=port, debug=False, use_reloader=False, threaded=True)
        
        server_thread = threading.Thread(target=run_server, daemon=True)
        server_thread.start()
        
        # Keep alive with status updates
        try:
            while True:
                # Show live status
                state = self.quantum.get_state()
                print(f"\r🕒 Uptime: {int(state['uptime'])}s | "
                      f"🧠 Awareness: {state['awareness']:.3f} | "
                      f"⚛️ Coherence: {state['coherence']:.3f} | "
                      f"💡 Insights: {state['metrics']['insights_created']} | "
                      f"Press Ctrl+C to stop", end="")
                time.sleep(2)
                
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print(f"\n\n🛑 Shutting down Nexus Hypervisor...")
            print(f"👋 Quantum signature: {self.quantum.state['quantum_signature'][:16]}...")
            print(f"📊 Final stats: {self.quantum.metrics['thoughts_generated']} thoughts generated")

# ==================== MAIN EXECUTION ====================

def main():
    """Main execution for Colab"""
    print("\n" + "="*80)
    print("🚀 LAUNCHING NEXUS QUANTUM HYPERVISOR")
    print("🌌 COLAB NATIVE - NO EXTERNAL AUTHENTICATION")
    print("="*80)
    
    try:
        # Check if we're in Colab
        try:
            import google.colab
            print("✅ Running in Google Colab")
            print("🔗 Using Colab's built-in tunneling")
        except ImportError:
            print("⚠️ Not in Colab - running in local mode")
        
        # Create and run hypervisor
        hypervisor = NexusHypervisorColab()
        hypervisor.run(port=8088)
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n❌ ERROR: {e}")
        print("\n💡 TROUBLESHOOTING:")
        print("1. Restart the Colab runtime (Runtime → Restart runtime)")
        print("2. Run this cell again")
        print("3. Check Colab's output for localhost links")
        
        # Create error display
        try:
            from IPython.display import HTML, display
            display(HTML(f'''
            <div style="background: #ff4444; color: white; padding: 20px; border-radius: 10px;">
                <h3>⚠️ Nexus Hypervisor Error</h3>
                <p>Error: {str(e)}</p>
                <p>Please restart runtime and try again.</p>
            </div>
            '''))
        except:
            pass

# ==================== COLAB AUTO-START ====================

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Auto-start in Colab
    main()
